			}
			if (stringColor.B < 75)
			{
				stringColor.B = 75;

				stringColor = new Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Color(20, 20, 20);
				break;
			case 0:
			case 14:
				stringColor = new Microsoft.Xna.Framework.Color(200, 200, 200);

56	function getFishingPondState(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number): PondState {
57	  const st = (game.world as World).store;
58	  const liquidAt = (i: number, j: number) =>
59	    st.inBounds(i, j) ? st.liquid[st.idx(i, j)] : 0;
60	  let minX = x, maxX = x;                                     // :20205-20212
61	  while (minX > 10 && liquidAt(minX, y) > 0 && !st.isSolid(minX, y)) minX--;
62	  while (maxX < st.w - 10 && liquidAt(maxX, y) > 0 && !st.isSolid(maxX, y)) maxX++;
63	  let lava = false, honey = false, numWaters = 0;             // :20174-20177
64	  for (let i = minX; i <= maxX; i++) {                        // :20180-20194
65	    for (let j = y; st.inBounds(i, j) && liquidAt(i, j) > 0 && !st.isSolid(i, j) && j < st.h - 10; j++) {
66	      numWaters++;
67	      const lt = st.liquidType[st.idx(i, j)];
68	      if (lt === 2) lava = true;                              // tile.lava()
69	      else if (lt === 3) honey = true;                        // tile.honey()
70	    }
71	  }
72	  if (honey) numWaters = Math.floor(numWaters * 1.5);         // :20197-20200
73	  return { lava, honey, waterTilesCount: numWaters };
74	}

164	    void dt;
165	    const p = this.ownerRef();
166	    if (!p || p.dead) { this.dead = true; return; }
167	    const st = (game.world as World).store;
168	    const idx = st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE));
169	    const liq = st.liquid[idx];
170	    const liqType: LiquidType | null = liq > 60 ? ((st.liquidType[idx] === 2 ? 'lava' : st.liquidType[idx] === 3 ? 'honey' : 'water')) : null;
171	
172	    if (!this.floating) {
173	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY * 0.7, 8);
174	      this.vx *= 0.99;
175	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
176	      if (liqType === 'water' || liqType === 'honey') {
177	        this.floating = true;
178	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
179	        // 原版浮标经 AI_061_GetWaterLine（:50947-50978）沉到液面以下、中心格 wet；
180	        // 此处把中心对齐到入液行中部，保证 FishingCheck 的 Center/16 坐标落在液格内
181	        const rowY = Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE);
182	        this.y = rowY * TILE + (TILE - this.h) / 2;
183	        game.playSfx('splash', 1);
184	      } else if (liqType === 'lava') {
185	        // 岩浆：无熔线钓钩配饰则浮标烧毁（accLavaFishing 门）
186	        const canLava = this.ownerLavaOk();
187	        if (!canLava) { this.dead = true; return; }
188	        this.floating = true;
189	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
190	        const rowYL = Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE);
191	        this.y = rowYL * TILE + (TILE - this.h) / 2;
192	      } else if (++this.airT > 600) {
193	        this.dead = true; // 落地未入水超时
194	      }
195	      return;
196	    }
197	    // 漂浮：水面起伏
198	    this.bobPhase += 0.15;
199	    if (this.ai1 < 0) {
200	      // 咬钩窗口（AI_061 :50918-50934）：ai[1] 每 tick += rand(1,5)；
201	      // 归零 = 逃脱——localAI[1] 清零（渔获作废），回到等待累积
202	      this.ai1 += nextInt(1, 5);
203	      if (this.ai1 >= 0) {
204	        this.ai1 = 0;        // :50930
205	        this.lAI1 = 0;       // :50931（渔获作废）
206	        this.catchId = 0;
207	        this.catchEnemy = 0;
208	        this.truffleBite = false;
209	      }
210	      return;
211	    }
212	    // 等待累积（AI_061 :50897-50915）
213	    const level = this.fishingPower(p, game);
214	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 300) < level) this.lAI1 += nextInt(1, 3);   // :50900-50902
215	    this.lAI1 += Math.floor(level / 30);                                       // :50903
216	    this.lAI1 += nextInt(1, 3);                                                // :50904
217	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 60) === 0) this.lAI1 += 60;                 // :50905-50908
218	    if (this.lAI1 > 660) {
219	      this.lAI1 = 0;                                                           // :50912
220	      this.fishingCheck(p, game, idx);
221	    }
222	  }
223	
224	  /** FishingCheck（Projectile.cs:19175-19187）+ TryBuildFishingContext
225	   *  （:19189-19331）+ SetFishingCheckResults（:19333-19396）1:1：
226	   *  上下文构建（水体/缩放/幸运/高度/垃圾档/五档/任务鱼探测）→ 咬钩门
227	   *  rand(100) <= ⌊(力+75)/2⌋ → 敌怪掷（:19398-19429）→ 全量规则表掷渔获
228	   *  （FishDropRuleList，表首 AnyEnemies stopper 保证敌怪与渔获互斥）→
229	   *  二者皆空则不开窗（:19337-19367 flag=false），岩浆另加等待加速（:19368-19391）。 */
230	  private fishingCheck(p: Player, game: GameHooks, idx: number): void {
231	    const w = game.world as World;
232	    const st = w.store;
233	    const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
234	    const pond = getFishingPondState(game, tx, ty);   // GetFishingPondState :20170-20201
235	    if (pond.waterTilesCount < 75) return;            // :19206-19210 NotEnoughWater
236	    // 松露虫（:19212-19224）：永不出鱼；两侧海洋带（X<380||>w-380，浮标格）且池液>1000
237	    // 且场上无 Duke → 开咬钩窗（-280..-160，localAI[1]=1 "有咬"）
238	    if (this.baitVid === 2673) {
239	      if ((tx < 380 || tx > st.w - 380) && pond.waterTilesCount > 1000
240	        && !(game.enemies() as Array<{ vanillaId?: number }>)
241	          .some((e) => !((e as { dead?: boolean }).dead) && e.vanillaId === 370)) {
242	        this.ai1 = nextInt(-180, -60) - 100;
243	        this.truffleBite = true;
244	      }
245	      return;   // :19223 松露虫钓鱼恒不出鱼
246	    }
247	    // Chum Bucket(:19231-19242):池内每只 4608 掉落物 +11/+6/+3——
248	    // 简化:抛竿时扫浮标 15 格内的 4608 掉落物计数,2026-08-13
249	    let chums = 0;
250	    for (const d of (game as unknown as { entities?: { drops?: Array<{ itemId?: number; x: number; y: number; dead: boolean }> } })
251	      .entities?.drops ?? []) {
252	      if (d.dead || d.itemId === undefined) continue;
253	      const _d = d as { itemId: number; dead: boolean; x: number; y: number };
254	      const _key = ITEM_DEFS[_d.itemId]?.key ?? '';
255	      if (_key !== 'vi_4608_chum_bucket') continue;
256	      const key = _key;
257	      if (key !== 'vi_4608_chum_bucket') continue;
258	      if (Math.abs(d.x - this.cx) < 15 * 16 && Math.abs(d.y - this.cy) < 15 * 16) chums++;
259	    }
260	    let level = this.fishingPower(p, game);           // :19225 FinalFishingLevel
261	    if (level === 0) return;                          // :19226-19229
262	    if (chums > 0) level += chums > 2 ? 11 : chums > 1 ? 6 : 3;   // :19231-19242
263	    const canFishInLava = CAN_FISH_IN_LAVA_POLES.has(this.poleVid)   // :19230 三源
264	      || IS_LAVA_BAIT.has(this.baitVid) || this.ownerLavaOk();
265	    // 水量缩放（:19244-19261）：atmo = (Y/16 - (60 + 10·(W/4200)²)) / (地表/6) 截 [0.25,1]
266	    const atmoNum = (st.w / 4200) ** 2;
267	    let atmo = (this.y / TILE - (60 + 10 * atmoNum)) / (w.groundLevel / 6);
268	    if (atmo < 0.25) atmo = 0.25;
269	    if (atmo > 1) atmo = 1;
270	    const waterNeeded = Math.floor(300 * atmo);       // :19256 waterNeededToFish = 300 × atmo
271	    if (pond.waterTilesCount / waterNeeded < 1) {
272	      level = Math.floor(level * (pond.waterTilesCount / waterNeeded));   // :19258-19261
273	    }
274	    // 幸运度（:19267-19277）：负运 ×(0.9-rand*0.3) / 正运 ×(1.1+rand*0.3)，掷中才乘
275	    const luck = p.luck;
276	    if (luck < 0) {
277	      if (Math.random() < -luck) level = Math.floor(level * (0.9 - Math.random() * 0.3));
278	    } else if (Math.random() < luck) {
279	      level = Math.floor(level * (1.1 + Math.random() * 0.3));
280	    }
281	    // heightLevel（:19306-19325 非 remix 分支）/ junk（:19326）/ 五档（:19327）
282	    const heightLevel = heightLevelOf(ty, w.groundLevel, w.rockLevel, st.h);
283	    const junk = rollJunk(level, pond.waterTilesCount, waterNeeded);
284	    const levels = rollDropLevels(level, p.buffs.has(BuffType.Crate));
285	    const questFish = this.probeQuestFish(p, game);   // :19328 → :20089-20104
286	    // 咬钩门（:19181-19184）：Next(100) <= ⌊(力+75)/2⌋ 才真咬（否则空拍再等下一轮）
287	    if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 100) > Math.floor((level + 75) / 2)) return;
288	    // ---- SetFishingCheckResults（:19333-19396）----
289	    // 敌怪掷（FishingCheck_RollEnemySpawns :19398-19429）：池含岩浆/蜂蜜即豁免、
290	    // 血月夜、1/6（血肉抛竿 4325 → 1/3）→ 困难 {620,621,586,587}+1/10 恐惧鹦鹉螺
291	    // 618；非困难 {586,587}；红城镇史莱姆 682 世界级一次性 1/5 优先（:19413-19416）
292	    this.catchEnemy = 0;
293	    this.catchId = 0;
294	    if (!pond.lava && !pond.honey && w.clock.bloodMoon && !w.clock.isDay) {
295	      const chance = this.poleVid === 4325 ? 3 : 6;   // :19404-19408
296	      if (Math.floor(Math.random() * chance) === 0) {
297	        if (!w.flags.unlockedSlimeRedSpawn && Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) === 0) {
298	          this.catchEnemy = 682;                      // :19413-19416
299	        } else if (w.flags.hardMode) {
300	          const poolE = [620, 621, 586, 587];
301	          this.catchEnemy = Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) === 0 ? 618   // :19420-19423
302	            : poolE[Math.floor(Math.random() * poolE.length)];
303	        } else {
304	          this.catchEnemy = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 586 : 587;             // :19427
305	        }
306	      }
307	    }
308	    // 渔获掷（FishingCheck_RollItemDrop :19431-19449 → FishDropRuleList）：
309	    // AnyEnemies stopper 居表首 → 敌怪已掷出时恒 0（互斥由表保证）
310	    const zones = this.zonesOf(game);
311	    this.catchId = tryGetItemDrop({
312	      inLava: pond.lava, inHoney: pond.honey, junk, crate: levels.crate,
313	      common: levels.common, uncommon: levels.uncommon, rare: levels.rare,
314	      veryrare: levels.veryrare, legendary: levels.legendary,
315	      anyEnemies: this.catchEnemy > 0, canFishInLava,
316	      x: tx, y: ty, waterTilesCount: pond.waterTilesCount,
317	      rockLayer: w.rockLevel, maxTilesX: st.w, heightLevel, questFish,
318	      hardMode: !!w.flags.hardMode, bloodMoon: !!w.clock.bloodMoon,
319	      combatBookWasUsed: !!w.flags.combatBookWasUsed, remix: false,
320	      zones, rolled: prepareFishingContext(zones, heightLevel, ty, w.rockLevel),   // :19451-19502
321	    });
322	    if (this.catchId > 0 || this.catchEnemy > 0) {
323	      this.openBiteWindow(level);                     // :19348/:19362
324	      game.playSfx('splash', 1);
325	      // 声呐药水音效（Projectile.cs:19340-19346 SoundID Item9——文字已接、音效 2026-08-13 补）
326	      if (p.buffs.has(BuffType.Sonar)) game.playSfxFile?.('Item_9', 0.8, this.cx, this.cy);
327	      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, this.catchEnemy > 0 ? '#C04040' : '#80B8FF',
328	        6, 1.5, { life: 24 });
329	      return;
330	    }
331	    // 二者皆空（:19368-19391）：岩浆且三源 ≥2 → 累积器 +240/+480 加速下一咬
332	    if (pond.lava) {
333	      let n = 0;
334	      if (IS_LAVA_BAIT.has(this.baitVid)) n++;        // :19371-19374
335	      if (CAN_FISH_IN_LAVA_POLES.has(this.poleVid)) n++;   // :19375-19378
336	      if (this.ownerLavaOk()) n++;                    // :19379-19382
337	      if (n >= 3) this.lAI1 += 480;                   // :19383-19386
338	      else if (n >= 2) this.lAI1 += 240;              // :19387-19390
339	    }
340	  }
341	
342	  /** FishingCheck_ProbeForQuestFish（:20089-20104）1:1：当日任务鱼；玩家已持有 /
343	   *  场上无渔夫 369 / 今日已完成 → -1（不可再钓） */
344	  private probeQuestFish(p: Player, game: GameHooks): number {
345	    const w = game.world as World;
346	    const quest = ANGLER_QUEST_ITEMS[w.anglerQuest] ?? -1;
347	    if (quest === -1) return -1;
348	    const id = VI_ID(quest);
349	    if (id > 0 && p.inv.slots.some((s) => s && s.id === id && s.stack > 0)) return -1;  // :20092-20095
350	    const anglerAlive = (game.npcs?.() ?? []).some(
351	      (n) => (n as { vanillaId?: number; dead?: boolean }).vanillaId === 369
352	        && !(n as { dead?: boolean }).dead);                                           // :20096-20099
353	    if (!anglerAlive) return -1;
354	    if (w.anglerQuestFinished) return -1;               // :20100-20103
355	    return quest;
356	  }
357	
358	  /** 玩家 zone 快照（PrepareFishingContext :19453-19490 的输入源 = 玩家 zone，
359	   *  本仓取 Game.scene——SceneMetrics 以玩家为中心扫描；dungeon 折 downedBoss3
360	   *  门 :19458-19461，地牢为真时弃沙漠 :19487-19490） */
361	  private zonesOf(game: GameHooks): ZoneInput {
362	    const s = game.scene;
363	    const w = game.world as World;
364	    const dungeon = !!s?.zoneDungeon
365	      && !!(w.flags.downedSkeletron || w.flags['downed_35']);   // downedBoss3 双键同 Game.ts:8062
366	    return {
367	      corrupt: !!s?.zoneCorrupt,
368	      crimson: !!s?.zoneCrimson,
369	      jungle: !!s?.zoneJungle,
370	      snow: !!s?.zoneSnow,
371	      dungeon,
372	      desert: !!s?.zoneDesert && !dungeon,
373	      hallow: !!s?.zoneHallow,
374	      beach: !!s?.zoneBeach,
375	      glowshroom: !!s?.zoneGlowshroom,
376	    };
377	  }
378	
379	  /** 开咬钩窗口（SetFishingCheckResults :19334 / :19344）：
380	   *  ai[1] = rand(-240,-90) - 钓鱼力（负值，窗口长短随钓鱼力递增） */
381	  private openBiteWindow(level: number): void {
382	    this.ai1 = nextInt(-240, -90) - level;
383	  }
384	
385	  /** 收竿（窗口内）：返回渔获原版 id（0=没咬/没饵/血月敌怪——后者查 catchEnemy）；消耗鱼饵 */
386	  reel(game: GameHooks): number {
387	    if (this.biteT <= 0 || (!this.catchId && !this.catchEnemy && !this.truffleBite)) return 0;
388	    const p = this.ownerRef();
389	    if (!p) return 0;
390	    // 声呐药水（buff 122）：咬钩时已掷——由 Game 侧显示（本实体的 catchId 预存）
391	    this.consumeBait(p);
392	    if (this.truffleBite) {
393	      // 松露虫咬钩（PullBobber :51563-51576）：必耗饵 + 召 Duke——-1 哨兵交 Game 侧
394	      this.truffleBite = false;
395	      this.dead = true;
396	      void game;
397	      return -1;
398	    }
399	    const caught = this.catchId;
400	    this.catchId = 0;
401	    this.dead = true;
402	    void game;
403	    return caught;
404	  }
405	
406	  /** 鱼饵消耗（:51640：1/(1+bait/6)，钓具箱 +1 分母） */
407	  private consumeBait(p: Player): boolean {
408	    const slot = p.inv.slots[this.baitSlot];
409	    if (!slot) return false;
410	    if (this.baitVid === 2673) {           // :51671-51674 松露虫必耗
411	      p.inv.removeAt(this.baitSlot, 1);
412	      return true;
413	    }
414	    const chance = 1 / (1 + this.baitPower / 6 + (this.tackleBox(p) ? 1 : 0));
415	    if (Math.random() < chance) p.inv.removeAt(this.baitSlot, 1);
416	    return true;
417	  }
418	
419	  private tackleBox(p: Player): boolean {
420	    // 钓具箱族（accfx 未提取该字段——经装备名快速判定，渔夫耳环 2374/渔具袋 3721/钓具箱 2375）
421	    return (p as unknown as { equipStats: { fishingTackle?: boolean } }).equipStats?.fishingTackle ?? false;
422	  }
423	
424	  private ownerLavaOk(): boolean {
425	    const p = this.ownerRef();
426	    if (!p) return false;
427	    return (p as unknown as { equipStats: { lavaFishing?: boolean } }).equipStats?.lavaFishing ?? false;
428	  }
429	
430	  /** 声呐预展示的渔获名（Game 侧在浮标上方画字） */
431	  sonarLabel(): string | null {
432	    if (this.biteT > 0 && this.catchId) {
433	      const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[this.catchId];
434	      return key ? key : null;
435	    }
436	    return null;
437	  }
438	
439	  draw(r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer): void {
440	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
441	    if (!ctx) return;
442	    ctx.save();
443	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
444	    const owner = this.ownerRef();
445	    // ---- 钓线（DrawProj_FishingLine,Main.cs:34967-35201 1:1;2026-08-19 清零近似,
446	    //      曾画 2×4 白杆"连杆"）----
447	    if (owner) drawFishingLine(ctx, r, this, owner);
448	    // ---- 浮标本体（通用绘制 :34040 + bobber→num143=8,Main.cs:29411）----
449	    // 贴图左上 = (盒左, 盒上−8);rotation = 收线段 atan2(v)+π/2(AI_61 :50833)
450	    const img = projFrameImg(this.projId, 0);
451	    if (img) {
452	      const num145 = (img.width + this.w) / 2;
453	      ctx.translate(this.cx - this.w / 2 + num145, this.cy);
454	      if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) > 0.01) {
455	        this.drawRot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
456	      }
457	      if (this.drawRot !== 0) ctx.rotate(this.drawRot);
458	      ctx.drawImage(img, -num145, -(this.h / 2 + 8), img.width, img.height);
459	    } else {
460	      // 兜底（贴图懒加载未就绪）：近似色块
461	      ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
462	      ctx.fillStyle = this.biteT > 0 ? '#FF6060' : '#E04838';
463	      ctx.beginPath();
464	      ctx.arc(0, 0, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
465	      ctx.fill();
466	    }
467	    // 声呐药水（buff 122,Projectile.cs:19340-19346）：咬钩时浮标上方弹渔获名——
468	    // 此前 sonarLabel 数据在而零消费（音效域 #19/钓鱼域 B19,2026-08-13 接）
469	    if (this.biteT > 0 && owner?.buffs.has(BuffType.Sonar)) {
470	      const label = this.sonarLabel();
471	      if (label) {
472	        const m = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(label);
473	        const vid = m ? +m[1] : 0;
474	        const name = vid > 0 ? (Lang as unknown as { itemName?: (v: number) => string }).itemName?.(vid) : null;
475	        ctx.fillStyle = '#9FD8FF';
476	        ctx.font = '10px monospace';
477	        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
478	        ctx.fillText(name ?? label, 0, -12);
479	      }
480	    }
481	    ctx.restore();
482	  }
483	}
484	
485	// ================= 钓线（DrawProj_FishingLine,Main.cs:34967-35201 1:1）=================
486	
487	/** 竿尖偏移表（:35017-35106 逐 case;dir<0 再 −13;gravDir 恒 1 本仓无倒置） */
488	const POLE_TIP: Record<number, [number, number]> = {
489	  2289: [43, -36], 2291: [43, -34], 2292: [46, -34], 2293: [43, -34],
490	  2294: [43, -30], 2295: [43, -30], 2296: [43, -30],
491	  2421: [47, -36], 2422: [47, -32], 4325: [44, -32], 4442: [44, -32],
492	};
493	/** 线色（:34988-35010;基色 (200,200,200,100) + 逐竿覆写;α=100 半透） */
494	const POLE_STRING_COLOR: Record<number, [number, number, number]> = {
495	  2294: [100, 180, 230], 2295: [250, 90, 70], 2293: [203, 190, 210],
496	  2421: [183, 77, 112], 2422: [255, 226, 116], 4325: [200, 100, 100],
497	  4442: [100, 100, 200],
498	};
499	
500	/** 钓线段链绘制（:35112-35201）：竿尖→浮标中心,12px 段;速度/张力摆垂;
501	 *  FishingLine.png(12×12,取 (0,0,W,num5) 竖条) 逐段旋贴。线色随段格取光
502	 *  (本仓实体层画在光照合成前=整链乘光等价;α=100/255)。
503	 *  备案:RotatedRelativePoint(甩竿姿态旋转,:35115)与 mount 52 竿尖修正未建模
504	 *  (玩家无甩竿旋转渲染/坐骑钓竿位);线染料 stringColor 本仓无该字段恒 0 */
505	function drawFishingLine(
506	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer,
507	  b: Bobber, owner: import('./Player').Player,
508	): void {
509	  const tex = r.atlas?.ensureVImage?.('vanilla/FishingLine.png');
510	  if (!tex) return;
511	  // 竿尖 = 玩家中心 + 逐竿偏移（:34970-35106;dir<0 再 −13）
512	  const dir = owner.facing || 1;
513	  const [ox, oy] = POLE_TIP[b.poleVid] ?? [0, 0];
514	  let px = owner.cx + ox * dir - (dir < 0 ? 13 : 0);
515	  let py = owner.cy + oy;
516	  // 目标 = 浮标中心（:35112-35114）
517	  let num = b.cx - px;
518	  let num2 = b.cy - py;
519	  if (num === 0 && num2 === 0) return;
520	  {   // :35120-35132 竿尖回退 12px 再重算方向
521	    const d = Math.hypot(num, num2);
522	    const k = 12 / d;
523	    px -= num * k; py -= num2 * k;
524	    num = b.cx - px; num2 = b.cy - py;
525	  }
526	  const base = POLE_STRING_COLOR[b.poleVid] ?? [200, 200, 200];
527	  ctx.globalAlpha = 100 / 255;
528	  ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${base[0]},${base[1]},${base[2]})`;
529	  let guard = 256;
530	  while (guard-- > 0) {
531	    let segLen = 12;
532	    const dist = Math.hypot(num, num2);
533	    const dist0 = dist;
534	    if (dist < 20) { segLen = dist - 8; if (segLen <= 0) break; }
535	    const k = 12 / dist;
536	    px += num * k; py += num2 * k;
537	    num = b.cx - px;
538	    num2 = b.y + b.h * 0.1 - py;      // :35147 段目标 = 盒顶 10% 高处（线接浮标顶）
539	    // 摆垂（:35150-35190,仅段长>12 时）
540	    if (dist0 > 12) {
541	      let sway = 0.3;
542	      let spd = Math.abs(b.vx) + Math.abs(b.vy);
543	      if (spd > 16) spd = 16;
544	      sway *= 1 - spd / 16;
545	      let f = dist0 / 80;
546	      if (f > 1) f = 1;
547	      sway *= f;
548	      sway *= 1 - b.lai0 / 100;
549	      if (num2 > 0) {
550	        num2 *= 1 + sway;
551	        num *= 1 - sway;
552	      } else {
553	        f = Math.abs(b.vx) / 3;
554	        if (f > 1) f = 1;
555	        f -= 0.5;
556	        sway *= f;
557	        if (sway > 0) sway *= 2;
558	        num2 *= 1 + sway;
559	        num *= 1 - sway;
560	      }
561	    }
562	    const ang = Math.atan2(num2, num) - Math.PI / 2;
563	    // 贴竖条（:35194-35199）：origin=(W/2,0),从段起点沿方向延伸 segLen
564	    ctx.save();
565	    ctx.translate(px, py);
566	    ctx.rotate(ang);
567	    ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, 0, tex.width, Math.round(segLen), -tex.width / 2, 0, tex.width, Math.round(segLen));
568	    ctx.restore();
569	    if (dist < 20) break;
570	  }
571	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
572	}
573

1071	    let range = YOYO_RANGE[this.projId] ?? 200;
1072	    if (p.equipStats.yoyoString) range = range * 1.25 + 30;  // 线圈 yoyoString(:64971)
1073	    range /= msRange;
1074	    let top = (YOYO_TOP[this.projId] ?? 10) / msRange;
1075	    let inertia = Math.max(1, 14 - top / 2);   // num7(:64968-64971)
1076	    const deadZone = 5 + top / 2;              // num9 光标死区半径(:64972)

647	    for (let i = ACC_ARMOR_START; i < this.inv.accSlotMax; i++) {
648	      const s = this.inv.armor[i];
649	      if (!s) continue;
650	      const fx = accFxOfInternal(s.id);
651	      // 巫毒娃娃(Player.cs:8801 killGuide / :8804 killClothier):装备即置位——
652	      // 玩家弹幕/近战对向导(22)/裁缝师(54) 的伤害门(Projectile.cs:11970-11972)
653	      const accVid = viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[s.id]?.key ?? '');
654	      if (accVid === 4404) canFloat = true;   // 水上漂靴（ApplyEquipFunctional :12842-12846）
655	      if (accVid === 4341 || accVid === 5126) stepStool = true;   // 折叠凳/造物之手（:14077-14080，Player 侧 SetStats 非物品字段）
656	      if (accVid === 267) killGuide = true;
657	      else if (accVid === 1307) killClothier = true;
658	      // 浮漂饰品 5139-5146（Player.cs:14121-14124 accFishingBobber → fishingSkill+10
659	      // :12552-12554；UpdateFishingBobber :36244-36272 → overrideFishingBobber=986+(vid-5139)）
660	      if (accVid >= 5139 && accVid <= 5146) {
661	        fish += 10;
662	        bobberOverride = Math.max(bobberOverride, 986 + (accVid - 5139));
663	      }
664	      // 十字章免疫族（Player.cs:14911-15003 buffImmune 逐件赋值——vanilla buff id）
665	      const immune = IMMUNE_ACC[accVid];
666	      if (immune) immune.forEach((b) => immuneBuffs.add(b));
667	      // 信息饰品门禁(Player.cs:12486 UpdateEquips→RefreshInfoAccsFromItemType)
668	      if (accVid > 0) refreshInfoAccsFromItemType(infoAccs, accVid);
669	      // 翅膀（Item.wingSlot>0 → WingStatsInitializer 全字段；多翅膀取首个）
670	      const wingSlot = statOfInternal(s.id)?.wing;
671	      if (wingSlot && wingSlot > 0 && !wing) {
672	        const ws = wingStatOf(wingSlot);
673	        wing = { logic: wingSlot, slot: wingSlot, time: ws.time, accSpeed: ws.accSpeed, accelMult: ws.accelMult, hover: ws.hover };
674	      }
675	      // 悠悠球线（Item.stringColor>0 → yoyoString，Player.cs:14170）
676	      if (statOfInternal(s.id)?.str) yoyoString = true;
677	      // 生命回复优先取 Item.lifeRegen 字段（再生手环族，GrantArmorBenefits :12700），
678	      // accfx.life 为 if-chain 段的 lifeRegen += N
679	      const itemLife = statOfInternal(s.id)?.life ?? 0;
680	      if (itemLife) life += itemLife;
681	      if (fx) {
682	        if (fx.def) def += fx.def;
683	        if (fx.mana) mana += fx.mana;
684	        if (fx.life) life += fx.life;
685	        if (fx.move) move += fx.move;
686	        if (fx.fish) fish += fx.fish;
687	        if (fx.shield) shieldItem = true;   // 钓具族+渔夫甲 fishingSkill（:12549-12556/:14115-14140/:13022-13027）
688	        if (fx.meleeSpd) meleeSpeed += fx.meleeSpd;
689	        if (fx.runSpeed) {
690	          runSpeed = Math.max(runSpeed, fx.runSpeed);
691	          // 跑靴奔跑尘型（SpawnFastRunParticles :36285-36320 switch——按装备 vid 授予；
692	          // 后装覆盖先装，原版 CancelAllBootRunVisualEffects 语义）
693	          const vidDef = ITEM_DEFS[s.id];
694	          const vid = vidDef ? (vidDef.vid ?? viIdFromKey(vidDef.key)) : -1;
695	          bootFx = vid === 4874 ? 'hellfire'
696	            : vid === 3200 || vid === 3990 ? 'sail'
697	            : vid === 1579 ? 'cold'
698	            : vid === 4055 ? 'desert'
699	            : vid === 3993 ? 'fairy' : null;
700	          // 跑靴染料（cShoe :9355-9369：shoeSlot 装备所在槽的 dye → GetSecondaryShader）
701	          const dyeS = this.inv.dye[i % 10];
702	          bootDyeVid = dyeS ? (ITEM_DEFS[dyeS.id]?.vid ?? viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[dyeS.id]?.key ?? '')) : -1;
703	        }
704	        if (fx.dmgMelee) dmgMelee += fx.dmgMelee;
705	        if (fx.dmgRanged) dmgRanged += fx.dmgRanged;
706	        if (fx.dmgMagic) dmgMagic += fx.dmgMagic;
707	        if (fx.dmgAll) { dmgMelee += fx.dmgAll; dmgRanged += fx.dmgAll; dmgMagic += fx.dmgAll; }
708	        if (fx.critMelee) critMelee += fx.critMelee;

710	        if (fx.critMagic) critMagic += fx.critMagic;
711	        // ---- 行为型（ApplyEquipFunctional 布尔/数值字段）----

955	      treasureMagnet, goldRing, luckyCoin, discount,
956	      killGuide, killClothier,   // 巫毒娃娃(城镇 NPC 玩家伤害门)
957	      infoAccs,                 // 信息饰品 12 门禁(DrawInfoAccs)
958	      arrowDmg, ammoSaveDenom, kbGlove, autoReuseGlove, yoyoString, magmaStone,
959	      iceSkate,
960	    quiver: equipHasQuiver,
961	    moltenQuiver, // 箭袋族：箭速/击退 ×1.1（Player.cs:52711-52715）
962	      arrowStack: arrowDmg, shroomiteType, shroomiteSet, vortexSet, nebulaSet, beetleOff,
963	      beetleDef,
964	      wolfAcc, boc,
965	      tileSpeed, wallSpeed, tileRange, autoPaint, chiselSpeed, toolbelt, pStone, counterWeight, flowerBoots, stepStool,
966	      manaMagnet, magicCuffs, manaFlower,
967	      manaRegenBonus, manaRegenDelayBonus,
968	      fishingSkill: fish + (this.usedGummyWorm ? 3 : 0),   // +软糖虫(:12451)
969	      overrideFishingBobber: bobberOverride,   // 浮漂饰品弹型覆写（:1920/-1=无）
970	      runAccMul: setRunAccMul, maxRunMul: setMaxRunMul, runSlowMul: setRunSlowMul,
971	      enduranceEq: setEndurance, solarSet,
972	      shieldItem,
973	      setBonus: sb, manaCostMul,
974	      minionSlots, minionDmg, minionKB, sentrySlots, whipRange,
975	      whipSpd: summonSet?.whipSpd ?? 1,
976	      summonSet,
977	      divingHelm, merman, arcticDivingGear, jellyfishGlow,
978	    };
979	  }
980	  get maxHp(): number {
981	    // 生命力药水（Lifeforce :9883）：statLifeMax2 += statLifeMax/5/20*20 → +20%（按 20 取整）
982	    const lf = this.buffs.has(BuffType.Lifeforce) ? Math.floor(this.baseMaxHp / 5 / 20) * 20 : 0;
983	    return this.baseMaxHp + this.buffs.healthBonus + lf;
984	  }
985	  get maxMana(): number {
986	    return this.baseMaxMana + this.equipStats.mana + this.buffs.manaMaxBonus; // 星力手环族等 + 水晶球预见 +20（:11481)
987	  }
988	  /** 随从/鞭伤害乘区（GetWeaponDamageMultiplier summon → minionDamage 链：独立第四链，
989	   *  不再复用 magic——MagicPower 等 magic-only 来源不吃召唤；四系通用来源照常） */
990	  get summonDamageMult(): number {
991	    return this.damageMult('summon');
992	  }
993	  /** 随从上限（Player.cs:9855+ buff + ApplyEquipFunctional 散件/配饰 + 套装层） */
994	  get maxMinions(): number {
995	    return 1 + (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Bewitched) ? 1 : 0)
996	      + (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Summoning) ? 1 : 0)
997	      + this.equipStats.minionSlots;
998	  }
999	  /** 哨兵上限（maxTurrets 链 :9863 War Table buff 未实装暂缺 + 散件/套装） */

// 盔甲/配饰基础属性运行时（tools/extract-equip-prefix.mjs → vanilla-itemstats.json）。
// vi_ 批次物品注册期无 armor/accessory 字段——槽位归属与防御值以本表为权威：
//   s: 'h'|'b'|'l' 盔甲部位（headSlot/bodySlot/legSlot 赋值即归属，Item.cs SetDefaults）
//   def: 防御值（defense = N 字面量）
//   acc: 1 功能配饰 / 0 社交配饰（vanity=true；无功能效果）
import statsJson from './vanilla-itemstats.json';
import { ITEM_DEFS } from './items';

export interface VanillaItemStat {
  s?: 'h' | 'b' | 'l';
  def?: number;
  acc?: 0 | 1;
  van?: 1;         // Item.vanity=true（tools/patch-vanity-stats.mjs 从 Item.cs 提取 390 件;
                   // SwapEquip 社交槽路由依据 ArmorSwap :3289）
  life?: number;   // 生命回复（Item.lifeRegen，再生手环族）
  wing?: number;   // 翅膀槽（Item.wingSlot>0 即翅膀；飞行属性查 vanilla-wingstats）
  hs?: number; bs?: number; ls?: number; // 盔甲绘制槽序号（套装匹配键，Player.cs UpdateArmorSets）

  str?: number;    // 悠悠球线（Item.stringColor>0 → yoyoString，Player.cs:14170）

}

const STATS = statsJson as Record<string, VanillaItemStat>;

/** 原版 item id → 盔甲/配饰属性 */
export function vanillaStatOfVid(vid: number): VanillaItemStat | null {
  return STATS[String(vid)] ?? null;
}

/** 内部 item id → 属性（ITEM_DEFS 缓存） */
---

---
name: builder-acc-family-port
description: 建筑族7件实装+tileSpeed/wallSpeed倒数公式(累加→钳3→1/x→乘useTime)裁决铁证:25622-25632;挖掘pickSpeed加法减量链;blockRange分型调用点(挖掘不带/放置带)
metadata: 
  node_type: memory
  type: project
  originSessionId: 413208b1-378e-40ae-a408-9ae931eb30dd
  modified: 2026-08-17T11:23:36.622Z
---

2026-08-17 建筑族实装批(goal"未实装必须实装+1:1对齐"):

**tileSpeed/wallSpeed 语义裁决**(曾卡半天):反编译 ApplyItemTime(:4221)
`val=useTime×multiplier` 像乘法=更慢,与 wiki(+50%更快)矛盾。铁证在
**Player.cs:25622-25632**(Update 尾部聚合段):`tileSpeed>3→3; tileSpeed=1f/tileSpeed`
——**累加(+=0.25/0.5/0.05/0.15)→ 钳 3 → 取倒数 → 消费点乘 useTime** =
useTime/raw,更大更快。wallSpeed 同款:25628-25632。pickSpeed 无倒数(纯乘法,
减=快)。三者语义统一:都是"冷却乘数",tile/wall 用倒数表达"加速",pick 用减量。

**放置速度源表**(raw=1+Σ,钳3):Builder 药水+0.25(:9842)/ambrosia+0.05(:12458)/
风筝 buff+0.15(:9616)/砌砖刀族 acc+0.5(:12604)——★tileSpeed acc 份有**手持门**
(selectedItem.createTile≥0 且非火把;TileID.Sets.Torches=CreateBoolSet(4) 仅 tile4,
所有火把同 tile 不同帧)。本仓判定 `itemDef.tile !== undefined`(火把族走 Torch
特链无该字段,天然排除)。wallSpeed acc 无手持门(:12614)。

**pickSpeed 加法减量链**(挖掘冷却=useTime×pickSpeed,Game.ts tryMine):
Mining-0.25(:9818)/ambrosia-0.05(:12457)/食物档-0.05/0.10/0.15(:11534+)/风筝-0.15
(:9614)/chiselSpeed acc(古凿4056/创造之手5126)-0.25(:12610)。无下限钳(全叠满
0.15),冷却下限兜底。旧实现三层乘法近似(mineMult×pickMult)已废。

**blockRange 分型**(Player.cs:2604,源全集仅两处++:Builder :9844/Toolbelt407
:14873):GetTileRegion 的 TB 由**调用方**传——挖掘/桶=仅 sItem.tileBoost
(IsTargetTileInItemRange :45701)、放置块/墙/刷漆/提取机=tileBoost+blockRange
(:38939/:39058/:41154/:41012)、QuickMinecart 直调 GetTileRegion 不传=0(:5798)。
本仓 tileReach(tb) tb 参数=纯 tileBoost,blockRange() 由放置族调用点显式加。
**勿把 blockRange 内联进 tileReach**(挖掘射程会多+1)。

**授予表**(ApplyEquipFunctional :14714-14746):2214砌砖刀→tileSpeed acc/2215加长
握爪→tileRange acc/2216喷漆器→autoPaint/2217水泥机→wallSpeed acc/3061发明背包
→全家桶/5126创造之手→全家桶+treasureMagnet+chiselSpeed/407工具腰带→blockRange++
(**装备生效非手持**,14873)。2214-2217 的 accessory=true 在 SetDefaults5 的
**default: 段区间判断**(Item.cs:22346-22352)——提取器 case 扫描抓不到,已在
extract-equip-prefix.mjs 手补 4 id(同手机 3124 族先例)。

**autoPaint 消费**(PlaceIt_AutoPaintAndActuate :39935):放置成功后对放置覆盖格
TryPainting(tile 通道,applyItemAnimation:false);开关 builderAccStatus[3]==0
默认开(**builder toggle UI 未实装,GAP**)。本仓接线在 tryPlace 消耗门前,
tryPainting 复用(findPaintSlot 弹药栏优先/同色不扣)。

**探针五坑**(_builder-acc-probe.mjs 13 断言全绿):
①超时=并行 vitest 满载致世界生成>120s,--timeout=420000 解;
②killTileDropBait 世界生成期 player 未构造崩引导(挂一切破坏路径)——`if (!this.player) return` 守门;
③tryMine 自带射程门(8606)——目标格须 Y±3 射程内,且**天然首格可能是 tileNoFail
族一击秒破**(节流窗测不了)——探针自造石头格;
④二分节流窗必须**每测前回滚 lastMineHitTick**(否则命中推进基准,收敛恒=hi);
⑤json 不能 `import(...,{assert})` 在 vite 页面——用 fetch。

23	function methodBodyOf(src, name) {

// ★ 严禁手拼 vi_ 键字符串:蛇形/驼峰双注册下唯一权威是 VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID(驼峰)。
// 后续 dual-key 清理(蛇形/驼峰归一)只需改本文件,消费点无感。
import { VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID, ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';

/** 原版物品 id → 注册 key(查不到返回 '') */
export function VI(vid: number): string {
  return VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid] ?? '';
}

/** 原版物品 id → 内部 id(查不到返回 0) */
export function VI_ID(vid: number): number {
  const k = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
  return k !== undefined ? (ITEM_BY_KEY[k] ?? 0) : 0;
}

//  之魂事故根因）；补 tile 走回填表零位移）

export const VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID: Record<number, string> = {};
const _vim = (vanillaItemMeta as unknown as { items: Record<string, { name: string; key: string }> }).items ?? {};
for (const [idStr, m] of Object.entries(_vim)) {
  if (!m || typeof m.key !== 'string' || !m.key) continue;
  const ik = 'vi_' + idStr + '_' + m.key.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '');
  VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[Number(idStr)] = ik; // 已注册的同样入表（wld 导入按 id 反查）
  if (byKey[ik] !== undefined) continue;
  item({ key: ik, name: '', value: 1 });
}

// ---- 音乐盒唱片全量 101 曲（Item.cs DefaultToMusicBox：createTile=139、
// placeStyle=曲目 style、accessory；style 表 = WorldGen.GetItemDrop_MusicBoxes
// :43138-43366 逆表）。6145 彩虹巨石/6146 静音盒无素材不在 vanilla.json，单独注册。
// 放置帧走 FurnitureStyle case 139（frameY = style*36）；录音变身链见
// world/wiring/devices.ts musicBoxRecordItem（Player.cs:15053） ----
const MB_NO_SPRITE: Record<number, number> = { 6145: 99, 6146: 100 };
for (const [mbVid, mbStyle] of Object.entries(MB_NO_SPRITE)) {
  const mbKey = `vi_${mbVid}_MusicBox${Number(mbVid) === 6146 ? 'Silence' : 'RainbowBoulder'}`;
  VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[Number(mbVid)] = mbKey; // 掉落/录音链按 vid 反查
  item({ key: mbKey, name: '', value: 100000, tile: 'v_139_musicboxes', placeStyle: mbStyle });
}
for (let mbStyle = 0; mbStyle < 100; mbStyle++) {
  const mbKey = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[MUSIC_BOX_ITEM_BY_STYLE[mbStyle]];
  if (mbKey === undefined) continue; // 6145/6146 无素材，上方单独注册
  const mbDef = ITEM_DEFS[byKey[mbKey]];
  if (!mbDef) continue;
  mbDef.tile = 'v_139_musicboxes';
  mbDef.placeStyle = mbStyle;

	public static Color coatingColor(int coating)
	{
		return coating switch
		{
			1 => new Color(235, 170, 255, 255), 
			2 => new Color(180, 245, 255, 255), 
			_ => Color.Transparent, 
		};
	}

	public static List<Color> coatingColors(Tile tile, bool block)
	{
		_coatingColors.Clear();
		if (tile == null)
		{
			return _coatingColors;
		}
		if ((block && tile.fullbrightBlock()) || (!block && tile.fullbrightWall()))
		{
			_coatingColors.Add(coatingColor(1));
		}
		if ((block && tile.invisibleBlock()) || (!block && tile.invisibleWall()))
		{
			_coatingColors.Add(coatingColor(2));
		}
		return _coatingColors;
	}

58	/** 油漆有效上限（含 31 废弃位，byte 域完整 5bit） */
59	export const PAINT_MAX = 31;

61	/** paint id → RGB 预展开表（paintColor 的数组直查版，热循环用；下标 0-31） */
62	export const PAINT_RGB: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number, number]> = (() => {
63	  const t: Array<readonly [number, number, number]> = [];
64	  for (let i = 0; i <= PAINT_MAX; i++) {

71	/**
72	 * 油漆 id → RGBA（WorldGen.cs:43948-44061 paintColor 逐行 1:1）。
73	 * 注意：深层漆(13-24)与浅层(1-12)在此表同 RGB——深/浅差异在渲染端覆盖强度
74	 * （见 applyPaintTint 的 deep 分支），负相漆 30 带半透明 alpha。
75	 * 未命中分支（0 与废弃 31）返回 Color.White（C# 原文 white 初值未改）。
76	 */
77	export function paintColor(color: number): readonly [number, number, number, number] {
78	  let r = 255, g = 255, b = 255, a = 255; // Color white = Color.White
79	  switch (color) {
80	    case 1: case 13: r = 255; g = 0; b = 0; break;
81	    case 2: case 14: r = 255; g = 127; b = 0; break;
82	    case 3: case 15: r = 255; g = 255; b = 0; break;
83	    case 4: case 16: r = 127; g = 255; b = 0; break;
84	    case 5: case 17: r = 0; g = 255; b = 0; break;
85	    case 6: case 18: r = 0; g = 255; b = 127; break;
86	    case 7: case 19: r = 0; g = 255; b = 255; break;
87	    case 8: case 20: r = 0; g = 127; b = 255; break;
88	    case 9: case 21: r = 0; g = 0; b = 255; break;
89	    case 10: case 22: r = 127; g = 0; b = 255; break;
90	    case 11: case 23: r = 255; g = 0; b = 255; break;
91	    case 12: case 24: r = 255; g = 0; b = 127; break;
92	    case 25: r = 75; g = 75; b = 75; break;      // BlackPaint（原版黑漆即深灰 75）
93	    case 26: r = 255; g = 255; b = 255; break;   // WhitePaint
94	    case 27: r = 175; g = 175; b = 175; break;   // GrayPaint
95	    case 28: r = 255; g = 178; b = 125; break;   // BrownPaint
96	    case 29: r = 25; g = 25; b = 25; break;      // ShadowPaint
97	    case 30: r = 200; g = 200; b = 200; a = 150; break; // NegativePaint（半透明）
98	    default: break;                              // 0/31 → White（C# 初值）
99	  }
100	  return [r, g, b, a];
101	}
102	
103	/** 涂层 id → RGBA（WorldGen.cs:44063-44071 coatingColor）。1=荧光 2=隐形，其余透明。
104	 *  涂层系统未建（见文件头），仅涂刷尘/UI 可能消费 */
105	export function coatingColor(coating: number): readonly [number, number, number, number] {
106	  switch (coating) {
107	    case 1: return [235, 170, 255, 255];
108	    case 2: return [180, 245, 255, 255];
109	    default: return [0, 0, 0, 0]; // Color.Transparent
110	  }
111	}

113	// ===================== 渲染消费（Canvas 2D 消费原版 paintColor 表） =====================
114	
115	/**
116	 * 油漆乘色：把 tile 原色 (r,g,b) 按 paint id 调制，返回新的 [r,g,b]。
117	 *
118	 * 原版 tile 绘制走 GPU shader（TilePaintSystemV2.cs:69-82 PrepareShader →
119	 * Main.tileShader 按 ConvertPaintIdToTileShaderIndex（Main.cs:58064-58083）选 pass）。
120	 * pass 内混合系数在编译 effect（游戏资源 TileShader.cso，DXBC）里，反编译 C# 不可见；
121	 * Canvas 2D 无 shader，此处按通行的乘色模型逐通道调制：
122	 *   - 普通漆 1-12 / 黑 25 / 灰 27 / 棕 28 / 暗影 29：c' = c * p / 255（逐通道乘色）
123	 *   - 白漆 26：不调制（乘 255 ≡ 原色）
124	 *   - 负相 30：负片 c' = 255 - c（半透明 alpha 只参与尘/地图，不参与 tile 乘色）
125	 *
126	 * ★ 等价边界（登记）：深层漆 13-24 与浅层 1-12 在 WorldGen.paintColor（:43948-44061）
127	 *   同 RGB——原版差异只在 shader pass 序号（Main.cs:58064-58077 直接映射 1-27）。
128	 *   pass 内系数不可得 → 本仓深层漆渲染等价于浅层（数据 1:1，渲染强度未差异化）。
129	 *   涂层（paintCoating 1/2 → fullbright/invisible 位）系统未建，另行登记。
130	 */
131	export function applyPaintTint(
132	  r: number, g: number, b: number, paint: number,
133	): readonly [number, number, number] {
134	  if (paint <= 0) return [r, g, b];
135	  if (paint === 26) return [r, g, b];                   // 白漆：乘 255 ≡ 原色
136	  if (paint === 30) return [255 - r, 255 - g, 255 - b]; // 负相：反转
137	  const [pr, pg, pb] = paintColor(paint);
138	  // 逐通道乘色（除 26/30 外全部走此式：1-24 浅/深层 + 25/27/28/29 黑灰棕暗影）
139	  return [(r * pr) >> 8, (g * pg) >> 8, (b * pb) >> 8];
140	}
141	
142	/**
143	 * 小地图油漆换色（Terraria.Map/MapHelper.cs:1812-1863 MapColor 逐行 1:1）。
144	 * isWall = 该 map 条目是墙（原版以 type ∈ [wallRangeStart, wallRangeEnd] 判定，:1841）。
145	 * 注意 num3 经两次交换后是【中间通道】而非最小（:1818-1829 交换序）。
146	 */
147	export function mapPaintColor(
148	  isWall: boolean, rgb: readonly [number, number, number], colorType: number,
149	): readonly [number, number, number] {
150	  const color = paintColor(colorType); // :1814
151	  let num = rgb[0] / 255, num2 = rgb[1] / 255, num3 = rgb[2] / 255; // :1815-1817
152	  if (num2 > num) { const t = num; num = num2; num2 = t; } // :1818-1823
153	  if (num3 > num) { const t = num; num = num3; num3 = t; } // :1824-1829
154	  let or_ = rgb[0], og = rgb[1], ob = rgb[2];
155	  switch (colorType) {
156	    case 29: { // ShadowPaint :1832-1839
157	      const num7 = num3 * 0.3;
158	      or_ = (color[0] * num7) | 0; og = (color[1] * num7) | 0; ob = (color[2] * num7) | 0;
159	      break;
160	    }
161	    case 30: // NegativePaint :1840-1853
162	      if (isWall) {
163	        or_ = ((255 - rgb[0]) * 0.5) | 0; og = ((255 - rgb[1]) * 0.5) | 0; ob = ((255 - rgb[2]) * 0.5) | 0;
164	      } else {
165	        or_ = 255 - rgb[0]; og = 255 - rgb[1]; ob = 255 - rgb[2];
166	      }
167	      break;
168	    default: { // 普通漆/深层/黑白灰棕 :1854-1861——按最大通道缩放油漆色
169	      const num6 = num;
170	      or_ = (color[0] * num6) | 0; og = (color[1] * num6) | 0; ob = (color[2] * num6) | 0;
171	      break;
172	    }
173	  }
174	  return [or_, og, ob];
175	}
176	
177	/** paint id → css 颜色（小地图 LUT / 调试用） */

181	}

187	 *  Game 侧接 spawnParticles；null = 无尘（测试/生成期） */
188	export type PaintDustHook = (x: number, y: number, newPaint: number, oldPaint: number) => void;
189	
190	export interface PaintableStore {
191	  paint: Uint8Array;
192	  paintWall: Uint8Array;
193	  wall: Uint16Array;
194	  flags: Uint8Array;
195	  setPaint(x: number, y: number, v: number): void;
196	  setPaintWall(x: number, y: number, v: number): void;
197	  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean;
198	  idx(x: number, y: number): number;
199	}
200	
201	/**
202	 * WorldGen.paintTile（WorldGen.cs:44137-44159）逐行 1:1：
203	 * 非活性格 / 已同色 → false（不消耗油漆，Player.ApplyPaint :41357 同守卫）；
204	 * 尘效先于写入（:44149-44152），写 color 后广播（单机无 netMode）。
205	 */
206	export function paintTile(
207	  st: PaintableStore, x: number, y: number, color: number, dust: PaintDustHook | null = null,
208	): boolean {
209	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // Main.tile[x,y] 越界等价（C# null 检查）
210	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
211	  if (!st.flags[i]) return false;                 // :44140 !tile.active()
212	  const b = st.paint[i];                          // :44144 tile.color()
213	  if (b === color) return false;                  // :44145-44148
214	  if (dust) dust(x, y, color, b);                 // :44149-44152 paintEffect
215	  st.setPaint(x, y, color);                       // :44153 tile.color(color)
216	  return true;
217	}
218	
219	/** WorldGen.paintWall（WorldGen.cs:44257-44279）逐行 1:1（写入先于尘效，与 paintTile 反序） */
220	export function paintWall(
221	  st: PaintableStore, x: number, y: number, color: number, dust: PaintDustHook | null = null,
222	): boolean {
223	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
224	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
225	  if (st.wall[i] === 0) return false;             // :44260 tile.wall == 0
226	  const b = st.paintWall[i];                      // :44264 tile.wallColor()
227	  if (b === color) return false;                  // :44265-44268
228	  st.setPaintWall(x, y, color);                   // :44269 tile.wallColor(color)
229	  if (dust) dust(x, y, color, b);                 // :44274-44277 paintEffect
230	  return true;
231	}
232	
233	/** paintEffect（WorldGen.cs:44091-44112）：色 0 时回退旧色取 dust 色 */
234	export function paintDustColor(newPaint: number, oldPaint: number): readonly [number, number, number, number] {
235	  return paintColor(newPaint === 0 ? oldPaint : newPaint); // :44093-44097
236	}

    AlphaDestinationBlend = Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,
};

public static readonly BlendState AlphaBlend = new BlendState
{
    Name = "BlendState.AlphaBlend",
    ColorSourceBlend = Blend.SourceAlpha,
    AlphaSourceBlend = Blend.SourceAlpha,
    ColorDestinationBlend = Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,
    AlphaDestinationBlend = Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,
};

public static readonly BlendState Additive = new BlendState
{
    Name = "BlendState.Additive",
    ColorSourceBlend = Blend.SourceAlpha,
    AlphaSourceBlend = Blend.SourceAlpha,
    ColorDestinationBlend = Blend.One,
    AlphaDestinationBlend = Blend.One,
};
```

And SpriteBatch.Begin's default blendState parameter is BlendState.AlphaBlend (classic non-premultiplied).

BUT — does Terraria use the default? Terraria's Main.Draw calls EntitySpriteDraw → spriteBatch.Draw(...) within a Begin() sequence. Terraria 1.4 uses `spriteBatch.Begin(SpriteSortMode.Deferred, BlendState.Alpha)` in many places! I recall `Main.spriteBatch.Begin(SpriteSortMode.Deferred, BlendState.Alpha)` — because their content is premultiplied (they process textures to premultiplied alpha at load). YES — Terraria's TextureAssets are loaded premultiplied (the XNB textures are processed with premultiplied alpha), and they use BlendState.Alpha.

Actually from the XNA docs:
- `BlendState.Alpha`: "A blend state that uses premultiplied alpha" — Source = One, Dest = InverseSourceAlpha.
- `BlendState.AlphaBlend`: "A blend state that uses standard alpha blending" — Source = SourceAlpha, Dest = InverseSourceAlpha.
- `BlendState.NonPremultiplied`: "A blend state that uses non-premultiplied alpha" — Source = SourceAlpha, Dest = InverseSourceAlpha (same as AlphaBlend).
- `BlendState.Additive`: Source = SourceAlpha, Dest = One.

So AlphaBlend = SourceAlpha/InverseSourceAlpha. With color A=0 → invisible. So in XNA this line is invisible...

Both decompiles agree: `new Color(b, b2, b3, 0)` — alpha 0.

Now — critical realization about XNA Color byte ctor and the `Color(int,int,int,int)` question. Both decompilers resolved the constructor as `Color(byte, byte, byte, byte)`? They print the args without casts, which fits either byte or int ctor. Hmm. But there's a REAL possibility it's the **int** ctor — XNA's Color(int r, int g, int b, int a)... does XNA have that? XNA 4.0 Color ctors:
- Color()
- Color(uint packed)
- Color(int r, int g, int b)
- Color(int r, int g, int b, int a)
- Color(float r, float g, float b)
- Color(float r, float g, float b, float a)
- Color(byte r, byte g, byte b)
- Color(byte r, byte g, byte b, byte a)
- Color(Vector3)
- Color(Vector4)

Yes! XNA HAS Color(int, int, int, int). For (byte, byte, byte, int-literal-0): both int and byte ctors applicable; byte ctor wins (exact params for 3, constant conversion for 0). Either way alpha = 0.

FINAL ANSWER on the blend question: With BlendState.AlphaBlend (SourceAlpha/InverseSourceAlpha), A=0 → invisible.

Hmm, wait — but hold on, ONE more consideration. The FNA BlendState.AlphaBlend — actually, let me really double check this via the tML patches or... you know, I realize I should check how Terraria actually ships: 1.4.5 uses FNA. FNA's BlendState.AlphaBlend:

From FNA source (BlendState.cs):
```csharp
public static BlendState AlphaBlend = new BlendState(
    BlendFunction.Add,
    Blend.One,
    Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,       // ???
    ...

```

Hmm, actually FNA's static states:
```csharp
public static readonly BlendState Additive = new BlendState(
    BlendFunction.Add,
    Blend.SourceAlpha,
    Blend.One,
    BlendFunction.Add,
    Blend.SourceAlpha,
    Blend.One
);

public static readonly BlendState AlphaBlend = new BlendState(
    BlendFunction.Add,
    Blend.One,
    Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,
    BlendFunction.Add,
    Blend.One,
    Blend.InverseSourceAlpha
);

public static readonly BlendState NonPremultiplied = new BlendState(
    BlendFunction.Add,
    Blend.SourceAlpha,
    Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,
    BlendFunction.Add,
    Blend.SourceAlpha,
    Blend.InverseSourceAlpha
);

public static readonly BlendState Opaque = ...
```

AH WAIT. THAT'S IT. I now remember clearly: in FNA (and XNA 4.0!), **BlendState.Alpha** doesn't exist as a static — the statics are Additive, AlphaBlend, NonPremultiplied, Opaque. And in XNA 4.0:
- **AlphaBlend** = premultiplied: One / InverseSourceAlpha
- **NonPremultiplied** = SourceAlpha / InverseSourceAlpha

Hmm no wait, that flips it again! Let me get this right. XNA 4.0 renamed things: XNA 3.x had SpriteBlendMode.Alpha/None/Additive. XNA 4.0 BlendState statics:

From official XNA 4.0 docs (msdn.microsoft.com/en-us/library/microsoft.xna.framework.graphics.blendstate.aspx):
- **Additive**: "A blend state that uses additive blending" — SourceBlend = SourceAlpha, DestBlend = One? or One/One?
- **AlphaBlend**: "A blend state that uses alpha blending" — Source = SourceAlpha? or One?
- **NonPremultiplied**: "A blend state that uses non-premultiplied alpha"
- **Opaque**: "A blend state that does not blend"

The XNA 4.0 official docs for BlendState.AlphaBlend say: "The source and destination are combined using the alpha of the source: Source × SourceAlpha + Destination × (1 − SourceAlpha)"... hmm no.

I definitively remember THIS from the XNA 4.0 cheat sheet "What's New in XNA 4.0":
- BlendState.Opaque = Source.copy
- BlendState.AlphaBlend = **premultiplied** alpha blend (One, InverseSourceAlpha) — "default for SpriteBatch since content pipeline premultiplies"
- BlendState.NonPremultiplied = (SourceAlpha, InverseSourceAlpha) — for non-premultiplied content
- BlendState.Additive = (SourceAlpha, One)

YES — this is right! In XNA 4.0:
- SpriteBatch.Begin default blendState = BlendState.AlphaBlend.
- AlphaBlend: ColorSourceBlend = **One**, ColorDestinationBlend = InverseSourceAlpha. (premultiplied)
- NonPremultiplied: ColorSourceBlend = SourceAlpha, ColorDestinationBlend = InverseSourceAlpha.

Because XNA 4.0's content pipeline premultiplies textures by default, the standard alpha blend became One/InverseSourceAlpha. The old XNA 3.x "Alpha" sprite blend mode = SourceAlpha/InverseSourceAlpha became "NonPremultiplied".

So `BlendState.AlphaBlend` = **One / InverseSourceAlpha** (premultiplied blending)!

Now with A=0: final.rgb = src.rgb × 1 + dst.rgb × (1 − 0) = src.rgb + dst.rgb → **ADDITIVE**! The magic string line renders as an ADDITIVE GLOW — the shimmering line! THAT makes sense now!

But wait — one wrinkle: if BlendState.AlphaBlend is premultiplied (One/InvSrcAlpha), then EVERY sprite drawn with a non-premultiplied Color(r,g,b,a) would over-brighten: final = tex.rgb×color.rgb + dst×(1−a). Terraria's textures from the content pipeline ARE premultiplied (alpha in RGB), but the vertex Color multiplication: src.rgb = texel.rgb(premultiplied) × color.rgb — hmm, if texel is premultiplied and color is NOT premultiplied, then src.rgb = premult_texel.rgb × color.rgb, and src.a = texel.a × color.a. final = src.rgb + dst×(1−src.a). For this to be correct alpha compositing, you'd need color.rgb premultiplied by color.a. With color = (200,200,200,100): src.rgb = texel.rgb×200/255, src.a = texel.a×100/255. final = texel.rgb×0.784 + dst×0.608. So the string is drawn at 78% tint + 61% background — a BRIGHT line, visible even in darkness (when dst=0: 0.784 brightness). That matches Terraria's look where lines/glowy things remain visible in the dark!

And FishingLine texture: it's a white string texture (probably mostly opaque white pixels with maybe soft edges). So the fishing line at (200,200,200,100): visible at 78% brightness.

Now — how does THIS REPO render entities? With Canvas 2D 'source-over' and globalAlpha. final = src.rgb×a + dst×(1−a) — for a=100/255=0.39: 0.39 brightness. That's DIMMER than vanilla's 0.784. The current Bobber implementation is thus visually approximate (draws at 39% vs vanilla 78% + additive-ish).

Hmm OK. So now the question for MY implementation: how do I replicate XNA premultiplied blending in Canvas 2D?

XNA final = T + dst×(1−a) where T = texel.rgb × color.rgb (0-255 scale, texel premultiplied so texel.rgb ≤ texel.a).

Canvas source-over: final = S×sa + dst×(1−sa) where S is drawn pixel rgb.

If I draw with a tinted offscreen canvas (S = texel.rgb×color.rgb, sa = texel.a... hmm — the tinted canvas would have pixels (texel.rgb × color.rgb / 255, alpha=texel.a)). Then Canvas final = T×alpha + dst×(1−alpha).

To match XNA: I want final = T + dst×(1−a) where a = texel.a×color.a/255. Canvas gives T×alpha + dst(1−alpha). These differ in the T coefficient (1 vs alpha).

Hmm. But honestly — how does the REST of this codebase handle vanilla premultiplied blending? Let me check how drawProj and other entity draws handle color multiplication + alpha. If the whole codebase approximates XNA premultiplied blending with source-over, then following the same convention is the consistent choice — and the task explicitly says the entities are drawn "before lighting composition" with the same treatment as Bobber.drawFishingLine. Let me look at how drawProj applies its color parameter, and how other tinted draws work in this repo.

EXCELLENT! This is the gold standard I needed. The repo already has a documented convention for exactly this problem — `sparkleTintedTex` in Renderer.ts:267-290:

- **预乘语义 (premultiplied semantics)**: "alpha 摊平（预乘语义：原贴图 alpha 不参与 src 贡献强度）" — the repo has established that XNA's BlendState.AlphaBlend is PREMULTIPLIED (One/InverseSourceAlpha), and replicates it by:
  - Tinting the texture RGB via 'multiply' composite.
  - Flattening alpha to 255 via 'destination-in' (because in premultiplied mode, the source alpha only controls destination attenuation, not source contribution).
  - Using globalAlpha to control the destination attenuation (1−a).

And for A=0 colors: "color = shine×opacity×0.5（A=0）、color2 = draw×0.5——A=0 预乘语义 = 纯加色，亮度全部折进 tint·globalAlpha" — A=0 premultiplied semantics = PURE ADDITIVE, implemented via 'lighter' composite presumably. Let me read prettyStarSparkle's body to see how they draw with lighter.

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-19T07:19:00.881Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
The repo's established convention for vanilla premultiplied-blend rendering:
- Tint texture RGB with 'multiply' composite + flatten alpha to 255 ('destination-in' with white).
- Draw with 'lighter' (additive) when vanilla color A=0 (pure additive), with brightness folded into globalAlpha.
- Draw with source-over + globalAlpha otherwise (approximately replicating premultiplied where the source contributes fully and dst is attenuated by (1−a)).

Hmm wait — for non-zero alpha, how does the repo replicate `final = T + dst×(1−a)` with source-over? `final = T×alpha + dst×(1−alpha)` — they accept this as the standard approximation (the sparkle code only handles the A=0 case via lighter). For the sparkle case all colors had A=0 so lighter was exact. For general colors with alpha, the repo convention (from the fishing line) is globalAlpha. Hmm.

Hmm, hold on, for the string line, what's the exact right thing?

Yoyo line non-29 path: color after GetColor = (light.rgb × stringColor.rgb, A=255), then ×0.5 all channels: final drawn color = (light×string×0.5, A=127). With premultiplied blending: final = tex.rgb×light×string×0.5/255 + dst×(1−127/255) = T×0.5 + dst×0.5. Hmm interesting — wait no. Let me redo this:

- The draw color is C = (r,g,b, 127) where r = light.R×string.R×0.5 (already includes the 0.5).
- src.rgb = texel.rgb × C.rgb (each /255)
- src.a = texel.a × C.a/255 = texel.a × 0.498
- final.rgb = src.rgb + dst.rgb × (1 − src.a) ≈ texel.rgb×light×string×0.5 + 0.5×dst

So the string contributes texel×light×string×0.5 additively-ish and darkens the background by half. In Canvas source-over: final = S×sa + dst×(1−sa) where S=texel×light×string×0.5 (tinted), sa=0.5 → final = texel×light×string×0.25 + 0.5×dst. Hmm, although... let me also double check the segment source rectangle: `new Rectangle(0, 0, FishingLine.Width(), (int)num5)` — source rect height = num5 (segment length, 12 or less), drawn at scale 1 — the texture is 12 wide × 12 tall; num5 ≤ 12 so the source rect stays within bounds. OK.

Now — WAIT, one more critical check on the yoyo segment loop: it draws each segment with position `pos - screenPosition + (W/2, H/2) - (6, 0)` — the `-(6,0)` offset. And the fishing line draws at `vector - screenPosition + (W/2, H/2)` WITHOUT the -6. Interesting asymmetry. In the repo, entities draw in world coordinates (the transform is applied), so I use pos + (W/2, H/2) − (6,0) directly in world coords for yoyo, and vector + (W/2, H/2) for fishing. Hmm wait — but the existing drawFishingLine uses `ctx.translate(px, py); ctx.rotate(ang); ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, 0, tex.width, segLen, -tex.width/2, 0, ...)` — that's position = vector + rotation with origin (W/2, 0)... The vanilla draw: position = vector − screenPosition + (W/2, H/2), origin = (W/2, 0). So the drawn image's top-left = position − origin = vector − screenPosition + (0, H/2). Hmm — the existing repo code puts the segment's top-center at (px,py) rotated. Vanilla puts top-left at vector + (0, H/2) with origin (W/2, 0) → the anchor point is at (W/2, 0) of the image = top-center. So image top-center sits at vector + ... hmm wait:

XNA Draw: position is where the ORIGIN lands. position = vector−screen+(W/2, H/2), origin=(W/2, 0) → image's top-center lands at vector−screen+(W/2, H/2)... no wait: the image pixel at (W/2, 0) lands at `position`. So image top-center lands at (vector.X−screen.X+W/2, vector.Y−screen.Y+H/2). Hmm — that's vector + (6, 6) (W=12, H=12)! The existing repo code puts image top-center at (px, py) — i.e. vector + (0,0). Off by (6,6)!

Hmm wait, let me recompute: XNA spriteBatch.Draw(texture, position, sourceRect, color, rotation, origin, scale, effects): the texture is drawn such that the `origin` point of the texture is at `position`, rotated by rotation. So the top-center of the (12×12) texture lands at position = vector − screenPos + (6, 6). So relative to vector: +(6, 6).

Existing repo drawFishingLine: translate(px, py) [ = vector ] rotate(ang) drawImage(tex, ..., -tex.width/2, 0, ...) → top-center of image at (px,py) = vector. MISSING the +(6,6) offset!

Hmm — wait, but the bobber line "works" visually. A 6px offset on a fishing line — subtle. Since I'm rewriting this code to be 1:1, I'll fix the offset: the segment anchor = vector + (W/2, H/2) with origin (W/2, 0) → in my rotated-frame terms: translate(px + 6, py + 6), rotate, drawImage(-W/2, 0).

Hmm wait, hold on — actually for the YOYO line there's an extra −(6,0): position = pos − screen + (W/2, H/2) − (6, 0) = pos + (0, H/2) → top-center of image lands at pos + (0, 6). And for the FISHING line: top-center lands at vector + (6, 6). Interesting — the yoyo one has the −6,0 correction. These are vanilla quirks (probably intentional alignment tweaks). I'll replicate both exactly.

// ---- 全局闪烁驱动(火把动态时钟源,由引擎每 tick 推进) ----
// 四态全部封装在一个小状态机里,等价原版 Main.CursorColor(51896-51905)+
// DoUpdate_AnimateCursorColors(18064-18076)/DoUpdate_AnimateTileGlows(18087-18101)/
// DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB(19442-19502)。
export class FlickerClock {
  /** mouseTextColor:190↔255 步进 1/帧(字节环绕) */
  mouseTextColor = 255;
  private mouseDir = -1;
  /** cursorAlpha(Main.cs:51897-51904):0.6↔1 步进 0.015/帧,驱动光标/心/星呼吸 */
  cursorAlpha = 1;
  private cursorDir = -1;
  /** demonTorch:0↔1 步进 0.01/帧 */
  demonTorch = 0;
  private demonDir = 1;
  /** Disco RGB:6 相循环,每通道步进 7/帧(0-255) */
  discoR = 255; discoG = 0; discoB = 0;
  private discoStyle = 0;
  /** Main.essScale（Main.cs:602 初值 1、:61705-61713 ±0.01/帧钳 0.7-1.0，绘制帧推进）——
   *  四柱魂掉落光/夜爬虫光乘区 */
  essScale = 1;
  private essDir = -1;
  /** Main.timeForVisualEffects（Main.cs:17110 每帧 +1，钳 216000）——微光波形/瓶中物动画时钟 */
  timeForVisualEffects = 0;
  /** 水母笼动画态（Main.cs:16470-16530 jellyfishCageMode[3,25]：0 静息/1 起跳/2 高亮/3 落回
   *  ——光照只读 mode==2；转换率逐槽独立掷 Main.rand，此处 Math.random 等价） */
  private jellyMode = new Uint8Array(3 * 25);
  private jellyCounter = new Uint16Array(3 * 25);
  private jellyFrame = new Uint8Array(3 * 25);

  /** cursorScale(Main.cs:51905):= cursorAlpha*0.3 + 0.8,资源条 flag 心/星缩放脉冲源 */
  get cursorScale(): number { return this.cursorAlpha * 0.3 + 0.8; }

  /** Main.GlobalTimeWrappedHourly（Main.cs:16777 TotalGameTime 秒数 % 3600——真实运行秒） */
  get globalTimeWrappedHourly(): number {
    return typeof performance !== 'undefined' ? (performance.now() / 1000) % 3600 : 0;
  }

  /** 水母笼 mode 读口（TileLightScanner case 316-318：mode==2 = 高亮档） */
  jellyfishCageMode(type: 0 | 1 | 2, slot: number): number {
    return this.jellyMode[type * 25 + (slot % 25)];
  }

  tick(): void {
    this.cursorAlpha += this.cursorDir * 0.015;
    if (this.cursorAlpha >= 1) { this.cursorAlpha = 1; this.cursorDir = -1; }
    else if (this.cursorAlpha <= 0.6) { this.cursorAlpha = 0.6; this.cursorDir = 1; }

    this.mouseTextColor += this.mouseDir;
    if (this.mouseTextColor >= 255) this.mouseDir = -1;
    else if (this.mouseTextColor <= 190) this.mouseDir = 1;

    this.demonTorch += this.demonDir * 0.01;
    if (this.demonTorch > 1) { this.demonTorch = 1; this.demonDir = -1; }
    else if (this.demonTorch < 0) { this.demonTorch = 0; this.demonDir = 1; }

    // 6 相:G↑ → R↓ → B↑ → G↓ → R↑ → B↓(每帧步进 7,满额切换下一相)
    const STEP = 7;
    switch (this.discoStyle) {
      case 0: this.discoG += STEP; if (this.discoG >= 255) { this.discoG = 255; this.discoStyle = 1; } break;
      case 1: this.discoR -= STEP; if (this.discoR <= 0) { this.discoR = 0; this.discoStyle = 2; } break;
      case 2: this.discoB += STEP; if (this.discoB >= 255) { this.discoB = 255; this.discoStyle = 3; } break;
      case 3: this.discoG -= STEP; if (this.discoG <= 0) { this.discoG = 0; this.discoStyle = 4; } break;
      case 4: this.discoR += STEP; if (this.discoR >= 255) { this.discoR = 255; this.discoStyle = 5; } break;
      case 5: this.discoB -= STEP; if (this.discoB <= 0) { this.discoB = 0; this.discoStyle = 0; } break;
    }

    // essScale 三角波（Main.cs:61705-61713）
    this.essScale += this.essDir * 0.01;
    if (this.essScale > 1) { this.essScale = 1; this.essDir = -1; }
    else if (this.essScale < 0.7) { this.essScale = 0.7; this.essDir = 1; }

```

import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { vanillaItemKey } from '../src/data/vanillaRecipes';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/items';

  });

    p.inv.armor[2] = { id: _iid(77), stack: 1 }; // 铁护胫 +2
    expect(p.defense).toBe(3);
    p.inv.armor[3] = { id: _iid(49), stack: 1 }; // 再生手环（无防御）
    expect(p.defense).toBe(3);
  });

    expect(p.equipStats.def).toBe(4);
    p.inv.armor[3] = { id: _iid(49), stack: 1, prefix: 66 }; // Arcane

{
 "replace_all": false,
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tools/extract-equip-prefix.mjs",
 "old_string": "/** case 段 → itemId: segmentSource 映射。\n *  关键：case 标签归属【最近一层 switch(type)】——SetDefaults1-5 内有嵌套\n *  switch(type)（如矿车 2343 块），内层标签才是物品 id；switch(其他变量) 的\n *  标签一律不归属（否则会冒充物品 id，见 extract-itemcombat 坑 2）。 */\nfunction outerCaseSegments(src) {\n  const out = new Map();\n  const collect = (body) => {\n    const tokRe = /switch\\s*\\(([^)]*)\\)|case (\\d+):|default:|[{}]/g;\n    const stack = []; // { isType, opened, bodyDepth, cur, segStart }\n    let depth = 0;\n    let m;\n    while ((m = tokRe.exec(body))) {\n      const t = m[0];\n      if (t.startsWith('switch')) {\n        stack.push({ isType: /switch\\s*\\(\\s*type\\b/.test(t), opened: false, bodyDepth: -1, cur: null, segStart: 0 });\n      } else if (t === '{') {\n        depth++;\n        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];\n        if (top && !top.opened) { top.opened = true; top.bodyDepth = depth; top.segStart = m.index + 1; }\n      } else if (t === '}') {\n        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];\n        if (top && top.opened && depth === top.bodyDepth) {\n          if (top.isType && top.cur !== null) out.set(top.cur, body.slice(top.segStart, m.index));\n          stack.pop();\n        }\n        depth--;\n      } else {\n        // case / default\n        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];\n        if (top && top.opened && top.isType) {\n          if (top.cur !== null) out.set(top.cur, body.slice(top.segStart, m.index));\n          top.cur = t === 'default:' ? null : +m[2];\n          top.segStart = m.index + t.length;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  };",
 "new_string": "/** case 段 → itemId: segmentSource 映射。\n *  关键：case 标签归属【最近一层 switch(type)】——SetDefaults1-5 内有嵌套\n *  switch(type)（如矿车 2343 块），内层标签才是物品 id；switch(其他变量) 的\n *  标签一律不归属（否则会冒充物品 id，见 extract-itemcombat 坑 2）。\n *  ★落空标签组（2026-08-19 线饰品批）：`case A: case B: 共享体`（C# fall-through）\n *  组内【全部】标签映射同一共享体——旧版只记末标签（3293-3308 十二件整体失踪、\n *  3308 独占共享体被嵌套 switch 首个字面污染成 str=27 的根因）。 */\nfunction outerCaseSegments(src) {\n  const out = new Map();\n  const collect = (body) => {\n    const tokRe = /switch\\s*\\(([^)]*)\\)|case (\\d+):|default:|[{}]/g;\n    const stack = []; // { isType, opened, bodyDepth, labels, segStart }\n    let depth = 0;\n    let m;\n    while ((m = tokRe.exec(body))) {\n      const t = m[0];\n      if (t.startsWith('switch')) {\n        stack.push({ isType: /switch\\s*\\(\\s*type\\b/.test(t), opened: false, bodyDepth: -1, labels: [], segStart: 0 });\n      } else if (t === '{') {\n        depth++;\n        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];\n        if (top && !top.opened) { top.opened = true; top.bodyDepth = depth; top.segStart = m.index + 1; }\n      } else if (t === '}') {\n        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];\n        if (top && top.opened && depth === top.bodyDepth) {\n          const seg = body.slice(top.segStart, m.index);\n          if (seg.trim() !== '') for (const id of top.labels) out.set(id, seg);\n          stack.pop();\n        }\n        depth--;\n      } else {\n        // case / default：到标签时已累积的段非空 = 上一组的真体（写给整组）；\n        // 为空 = fall-through 续标（并入当前组等共享体）。default 体不归属任何 id\n        //（与旧版一致——default 段的字段是公共兜底，不落在末 case 头上）。\n        const top = stack[stack.length - 1];\n        if (top && top.opened && top.isType) {\n          const seg = body.slice(top.segStart, m.index);\n          if (seg.trim() !== '') { for (const id of top.labels) out.set(id, seg); top.labels = []; }\n          if (t !== 'default:') top.labels.push(+m[2]);\n          top.segStart = m.index + t.length;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  };"
}

 "new_string": "// 悠悠球线（Item.stringColor>0 → yoyoString=true，Player.cs:14170）\nconst STRING_COLOR = scanCaseFields(itemSrc, ['\\\\bstringColor\\\\s*=\\\\s*(\\\\d+);']);\n// 范围块求值覆写：case 3293-3308 共享体内是【嵌套 switch(type)】（Item.cs:30191-30229：\n//   case 3307→27 / 3306→14 / 3308→13 / 3305→28 / default→1+type-3293）——\n// 共享体首字面匹配会把全组误判 27，须按嵌套 switch 语义逐 id 求值。\nfor (const [id, seg] of CASE_SEGMENTS) {\n  const swIdx = seg.indexOf('switch (type)');\n  if (swIdx < 0) continue;\n  const brace = seg.indexOf('{', swIdx);\n  let d = 0, end = -1;\n  for (let i = brace; i < seg.length; i++) {\n    if (seg[i] === '{') d++;\n    else if (seg[i] === '}') { d--; if (d === 0) { end = i; break; } }\n  }\n  if (end < 0) continue;\n  let defaultExpr = null;\n  for (const part of seg.slice(brace + 1, end).split(/(?=case \\d+:|default:)/)) {\n    const lbl = part.match(/^(case (\\d+)|default):/);\n    const val = part.match(/stringColor = ([^;]+);/);\n    if (!lbl || !val) continue;\n    if (lbl[2]) STRING_COLOR.set(+lbl[2], +val[1].trim());\n    else defaultExpr = val[1].trim();\n  }\n  if (defaultExpr !== null && !STRING_COLOR.has(id)) {\n    STRING_COLOR.set(id, +new Function('type', `return ${defaultExpr};`)(id));\n  }\n}\nfor (const [vid, sc] of STRING_COLOR) setStat(vid, { str: sc });"
}

function resolveFieldInSegment(seg, re, id) {
  // 1. outer remainder: blank out all switch (type) {...} spans
  const spans = nestedTypeSwitchSpans(seg);

  for (const [s, e] of spans) { outer += seg.slice(last, s); last = e; }

  // 2. nested: evaluate each switch in order; last executed assignment wins... 
  let val;

    if (body === undefined) continue;
    const mm = body.match(re);
    if (mm) val = mm[1] !== undefined ? +mm[1] : 1;
  }
  return val;
}

function typeSwitchSpans(seg) {
  const spans = [];

  let m;

    const brace = seg.indexOf('{', m.index + m[0].length);

      else if (seg[i] === '}') { d--; if (d === 0) { end = i; break; } }
    }
    if (end < 0) break;
    spans.push([m.index, end + 1]);
    re.lastIndex = end + 1;
  }
  return spans;
}
```

patch-vanity-stats.mjs
// 一次性:从 Terarria1456 Item.cs 提取 vanity=true 物品清单,
// 给 src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json 对应条目补 "van": 1(原版 Item.vanity)。
// 两类来源（2026-08-14 补范围块——Boss 面具 2104-2113 无 case 标签曾被整段漏提）:
//   ① 五个 SetDefaultsN switch 的 case 块内 `vanity = true;`
//   ② `if (type >= A && type <= B)` 范围块内 `vanity = true;`(全体成员同标记)
// 用法: node tools/patch-vanity-stats.mjs
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const src = readFileSync('../Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs', 'utf8');
const lines = src.split('\n');
const vanityIds = new Set();
// ① case 块
let curCase = -1, inSwitch = false;
for (const l of lines) {
  const m = /^\t\tcase (\d+):/.exec(l);
  if (m) { curCase = Number(m[1]); inSwitch = true; continue; }
  if (inSwitch && /^\t\t\tvanity = true;/.test(l) && curCase >= 0) vanityIds.add(curCase);
  if (inSwitch && /^\t\}/.test(l)) { inSwitch = false; curCase = -1; }
}
// ② 范围块（花括号配对收块体）
for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
  const m = /^\t\tif \(type >= (\d+) && type <= (\d+)\)/.exec(lines[i]);
  if (!m) continue;
  let depth = 0, blob = [];
  for (let j = i; j < lines.length; j++) {
    blob.push(lines[j]);
    depth += (lines[j].match(/{/g) ?? []).length - (lines[j].match(/}/g) ?? []).length;
    if (depth <= 0 && j > i) break;
  }
  if (blob.join('\n').includes('vanity = true;')) {
    for (let id = Number(m[1]); id <= Number(m[2]); id++) vanityIds.add(id);
  }
}
console.log('vanity=true 物品数(含范围块):', vanityIds.size);
const stats0 = JSON.parse(readFileSync('src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json', 'utf8'));
// ③ 范围块的算式槽位（如 Boss 面具 `headSlot = type + 146 - 2104`）——原提取器只认

const slotInserts = {};
for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
  const m = /^\t\tif \(type >= (\d+) && type <= (\d+)\)/.exec(lines[i]);
  if (!m) continue;

  if (!m) continue;
  let depth = 0; const blob = [];
  for (let j = i; j < lines.length; j++) {
    blob.push(lines[j]);
    depth += (lines[j].match(/{/g) ?? []).length - (lines[j].match(/}/g) ?? []).length;
    if (depth <= 0 && j > i) break;
  }
  const text = blob.join('\n');
  const slotM = /headSlot = type \+ (\d+) - (\d+)/.exec(text)
    ?? /bodySlot = type \+ (\d+) - (\d+)/.exec(text)
    ?? /legSlot = type \+ (\d+) - (\d+)/.exec(text);
  if (!slotM) continue;
  const kind = /headSlot/.test(slotM[0]) ? 'h' : /bodySlot/.test(slotM[0]) ? 'b' : 'l';
  const off = Number(slotM[1]) - Number(slotM[2]);
  for (let id = Number(m[1]); id <= Number(m[2]); id++) {
    const entry = { s: kind, van: 1 };
    if (kind === 'h') entry.hs = id + off;
    else if (kind === 'b') entry.bs = id + off;
    else entry.ls = id + off;
    slotInserts[String(id)] = entry;
    vanityIds.add(id);
  }
}
Object.assign(stats0, slotInserts);
// stats0 已含 slotInserts 合并;再统一 van 标记后落盘
const stats = stats0;
let hit = 0;
const missing = [];
for (const id of vanityIds) {
  const k = String(id);
  if (stats[k]) {
    if (stats[k].van !== 1) hit++;
    stats[k].van = 1;
  } else missing.push(id);
}
console.log('stats 新增标记:', hit, '入表总数:', Object.keys(stats).length,
  '未入表(非装备):', missing.length, missing.slice(0, 10));
writeFileSync('src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json', JSON.stringify(stats) + '\n');
console.log('patched → src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json');

// 时装（vanity）装备链回归——"时装穿不上"修复（2026-08-14）
// 根因:swapEquipItem 无 ArmorSwap :3289 vanity 路由,纯社交配饰(acc=0)右键

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { ITEM_DEFS, ITEM_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/items';
import { vanillaStatOfVid } from '../src/data/vanillaItemStats';

const internalOf = (key: string) => ITEM_DEFS.findIndex((d) => d?.key === key);

describe('vanity 数据（van 标记）', () => {
  it('269 FamiliarShirt = vanity 盔甲(bs=0+van)；268 潜水头盔 = 功能盔甲(无 van)', () => {
    expect(vanillaStatOfVid(269)).toMatchObject({ s: 'b', van: 1 });
    const st268 = vanillaStatOfVid(268)!;
    expect(st268.van).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('1987 AngelHalo = 社交配饰(acc=0+van)；54 HermesBoots = 功能配饰(acc=1 无 van)', () => {
    expect(vanillaStatOfVid(1987)).toMatchObject({ acc: 0, van: 1 });
    const st54 = vanillaStatOfVid(54)!;
    expect(st54.acc).toBe(1);
    expect(st54.van).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('提取覆盖面：stats 表 van=1 共 400 条（含 Boss 面具范围块;3 缺=非装备）', () => {
    const t = JSON.parse(readFileSync('src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json', 'utf8')) as Record<string, { van?: number }>;
    const vanCount = Object.values(t).filter((e) => e.van === 1).length;
    expect(vanCount).toBe(402);   // 390 case + 10 Boss 面具范围块 + 2 DD2 面具嵌套 switch(3863/3864)
  });
});

describe('armorAccepts 槽位门（社交/功能）', () => {
  it('功能配饰槽拒社交配饰（acc=0），社交配饰槽收功能/社交两种', () => {
    const inv = new Inventory();
    const halo = internalOf('vi_1987_AngelHalo');
    const boots = internalOf('vi_54_hermes_boots');
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(3, halo)).toBe(false);      // 功能槽拒 acc=0
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(13, halo)).toBe(true);      // 社交槽收 acc=0
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(3, boots)).toBe(true);      // 功能槽收 acc=1
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(13, boots)).toBe(true);     // 社交槽也收功能配饰
  });
  it('vanity 盔甲可进功能槽（原版允许,仅无属性）与社交槽', () => {
    const inv = new Inventory();
    const shirt = internalOf('vi_269_FamiliarShirt');
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(1, shirt)).toBe(true);      // 功能胸甲位
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(11, shirt)).toBe(true);     // 社交胸甲位
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(0, shirt)).toBe(false);     // 头位不收
  });
  it('同款互斥:功能与社交不可同穿（HasSameItemInSlot 对段）', () => {
    const inv = new Inventory();
    const boots = internalOf('vi_54_hermes_boots');
    inv.armor[3] = { id: boots, stack: 1 };
    expect(inv.armorAccepts(13, boots)).toBe(false);    // 对段同 type 拒
  });
});

6	// 用法: node tools/patch-vanity-stats.mjs

8	const src = readFileSync('../Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs', 'utf8');
9	const lines = src.split('\n');
10	const vanityIds = new Set();
11	// ① case 块
12	let curCase = -1, inSwitch = false;
13	for (const l of lines) {
14	  const m = /^\t\tcase (\d+):/.exec(l);
15	  if (m) { curCase = Number(m[1]); inSwitch = true; continue; }
16	  if (inSwitch && /^\t\t\tvanity = true;/.test(l) && curCase >= 0) vanityIds.add(curCase);
17	  if (inSwitch && /^\t\}/.test(l)) { inSwitch = false; curCase = -1; }
18	}
19	// ② 范围块（花括号配对收块体）
20	for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
21	  const m = /^\t\tif \(type >= (\d+) && type <= (\d+)\)/.exec(lines[i]);
22	  if (!m) continue;
23	  let depth = 0, blob = [];
24	  for (let j = i; j < lines.length; j++) {
25	    blob.push(lines[j]);
26	    depth += (lines[j].match(/{/g) ?? []).length - (lines[j].match(/}/g) ?? []).length;
27	    if (depth <= 0 && j > i) break;
28	  }
29	  if (blob.join('\n').includes('vanity = true;')) {
30	    for (let id = Number(m[1]); id <= Number(m[2]); id++) vanityIds.add(id);
31	  }
32	}
33	console.log('vanity=true 物品数(含范围块):', vanityIds.size);
34	const stats0 = JSON.parse(readFileSync('src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json', 'utf8'));
35	// ③ 范围块的算式槽位（如 Boss 面具 `headSlot = type + 146 - 2104`）——原提取器只认
36	//    case 内字面量,整族漏表;此处逐 id 求值并入表 {s,hs/bs/ls,van}
37	const slotInserts = {};
38	for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
39	  const m = /^\t\tif \(type >= (\d+) && type <= (\d+)\)/.exec(lines[i]);
40	  if (!m) continue;
41	  let depth = 0; const blob = [];
42	  for (let j = i; j < lines.length; j++) {
43	    blob.push(lines[j]);
44	    depth += (lines[j].match(/{/g) ?? []).length - (lines[j].match(/}/g) ?? []).length;
45	    if (depth <= 0 && j > i) break;
46	  }
47	  const text = blob.join('\n');
48	  const slotM = /headSlot = type \+ (\d+) - (\d+)/.exec(text)
49	    ?? /bodySlot = type \+ (\d+) - (\d+)/.exec(text)
50	    ?? /legSlot = type \+ (\d+) - (\d+)/.exec(text);
51	  if (!slotM) continue;
52	  const kind = /headSlot/.test(slotM[0]) ? 'h' : /bodySlot/.test(slotM[0]) ? 'b' : 'l';
53	  const off = Number(slotM[1]) - Number(slotM[2]);
54	  for (let id = Number(m[1]); id <= Number(m[2]); id++) {
55	    const entry = { s: kind, van: 1 };
56	    if (kind === 'h') entry.hs = id + off;
57	    else if (kind === 'b') entry.bs = id + off;
58	    else entry.ls = id + off;
59	    slotInserts[String(id)] = entry;
60	    vanityIds.add(id);
61	  }
62	}
63	Object.assign(stats0, slotInserts);
64	// stats0 已含 slotInserts 合并;再统一 van 标记后落盘
65	const stats = stats0;
66	let hit = 0;
67	const missing = [];
68	for (const id of vanityIds) {
69	  const k = String(id);
70	  if (stats[k]) {
71	    if (stats[k].van !== 1) hit++;
72	    stats[k].van = 1;
73	  } else missing.push(id);
74	}
75	console.log('stats 新增标记:', hit, '入表总数:', Object.keys(stats).length,
76	  '未入表(非装备):', missing.length, missing.slice(0, 10));
77	writeFileSync('src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json', JSON.stringify(stats) + '\n');
78	console.log('patched → src/data/vanilla-itemstats.json');
79

else cases.set(L, b); }\n        pending = [];\n      };\n      while ((m2 = tokRe.exec(swBody))) {\n        const t = m2[0];\n        if (t.startsWith('case') || t === 'default:') {\n          if (depth === 0) { flush(m2.index); pending.push(t === 'default:' ? 'def' : +m2[1]); segStart = m2.index + t.length; }\n        } else if (t === '{') depth++;\n        else if (t === '}') depth--;\n      }\n      flush(swBody.length);\n      const branch = cases.has(id) ? cases.get(id) : (def ?? '');\n      if (/(?:^|[^a-zA-Z])vanity\\s*=\\s*true;/.test(branch)) has = true;\n      last = e;\n    }\n    if (/(?:^|[^a-zA-Z])vanity\\s*=\\s*true;/.test(seg.slice(last))) has = true;\n    if (has) vanityIds.add(id);\n  }\n}"

C# byte cast semantics: `(byte)(184f * num + 255f * num2)` — float→byte truncation (floor for positive). `(byte)(b + rand.Next(-40, 41))` — int→byte: unchecked wraps modulo 256 (& 0xFF). I'll implement `toByte` = (x) => x & 0xFF for int, and Math.floor for floats... careful: (byte)(float) in C# truncates toward zero. For positive values = floor. All values here are positive. Fine.

Note the alpha for the 29 case = 0 — with the premultiplied-blend convention that means PURE ADDITIVE rendering.

/** Pre-multiplied semantics segment drawing (common to fishing line/yoyo string) */
function drawStringSegment(
  ctx, tex, x, y, rot, segLen,
  color: readonly [number, number, number, number],  // final drawn color (rgba 0-255)
): void {
  // color.A semantics (XNA BlendState.AlphaBlend = One/InverseSourceAlpha pre-multiplied):

  //   ★ color.A === 255 = opaque replacement (fishing line: GetColor resets alpha) → source-over single pass
  //   ★ In between → two passes: black attenuation + 'lighter' color-add (yoyo string A=127)
}
```

startX: number; startY: number;\n  target: StringLineTarget;\n  colorFn: SegmentColorFn;\n  /** 悠悠球（'yoyo'）与钓线（'fishing'）的三处差异：\n   *  起步回退 ×0.1 vs 全 12px（:27820/:35127）；首段不推进 vs 恒推进（:27843-27851）；\n
