同构循环）：\n *  12px 段沿起点→目标推进，速度/张力摆垂（num8），逐段旋贴 FishingLine 竖条。\n *  guard 上限为防御性护栏（原版无；终止条件 = 段距 <20 走尾段）。 */\nfunction runSegmentChain(a: ChainArgs): void {\n  const { ctx, tex, target, colorFn, variant } = a;\n  const tw = (tex as { width: number }).width;\n  const th = (tex as { height: number }).height;\n  let px = a.startX, py = a.startY;\n  let num = target.x + target.w * 0.5 - px;               // :27804/:35112 目标=盒中心\n  let num2 = target.y + target.h * 0.5 - py;\n  let flag = true;\n  let flag2 = true;                                       // :27809 悠悠球首段不推进\n  if (num === 0 && num2 === 0) {\n    flag = false;                                         // :27810/:35117\n  } else {\n    const k = 12 / Math.hypot(num, num2);                 // :27816-27819/:35123-35126\n    num *= k; num2 *= k;\n    if (variant === 'yoyo') {                             // :27820-27821 起步回退 ×0.1\n      px -= num * 0.1; py -= num2 * 0.1;\n    } else {                                              // :35127-35128 全 12px 回退\n      px -= num; py -= num2;\n    }\n    num = target.x + target.w * 0.5 - px;\n    num2 = target.y + target.h * 0.5 - py;\n  }\n  let guard = 512;\n  while (flag && guard-- > 0) {\n    let segLen = 12;                                      // :27827/:35134\n    const dist = Math.hypot(num, num2);\n    const dist0 = dist;                                   // num7\n    if (Number.isNaN(dist)) { flag = false; continue; }   // :27830-27834/:35137-35141\n    if (dist < 20) { segLen = dist - 8; flag = false; }   // :27835-27839/:35142-35146\n    const k = 12 / dist;\n    num *= k; num2 *= k;\n    if (variant === 'yoyo') {                             // :27843-27851 首段不推进\n      if (flag2) flag2 = false;\n      else { px += num; py += num2; }\n    } else {                                              // :35150-35151 恒推进\n      px += num; py += num2;\n    }\n    num = target.x + target.w * 0.5 - px;\n    num2 = target.y + target.h * 0.1 - py;                // :27852-27853/:35152-35153 段目标=盒顶 10%\n    if (dist0 > 12) {                                     // :27854/:35154 摆垂（仅段距 >12）\n      let sway = 0.3;\n      let spd = Math.abs(target.vx) + Math.abs(target.vy);\n      if (spd > 16) spd = 16;\n      sway *= 1 - spd / 16;                               // :27862-27863\n      let f = dist0 / 80;\n      if (f > 1) f = 1;\n      sway *= f;                                          // :27869/:35169\n      if (sway < 0) sway = 0;                             // :27870-27873\n      if (variant === 'yoyo') {\n        sway *= f;                                        // :27874 悠悠球二次 ×f\n        sway *= 0.5;                                      // :27875 ×0.5\n      } else {\n        sway *= 1 - (target.lai0 ?? 0) / 100;             // :35174-35175 张力收敛\n      }\n      if (num2 > 0) {                                     // :27876-27880/:35176-35180\n        num2 *= 1 + sway;\n        num *= 1 - sway;\n      } else {                                            // :27881-27896/:35181-35196\n        f = Math.abs(target.vx) / 3;\n        if (f > 1) f = 1;\n        f -= 0.5;\n        sway *= f;\n        if (sway > 0) sway *= 2;\n        num2 *= 1 + sway;\n        num *= 1 - sway;\n      }\n    }\n    const rot = Math.atan2(num2, num) - Math.PI / 2;      // :27898/:35198\n    // origin 落点（XNA position−screenPos+(W/2,H/2)：悠悠球线再 −(6,0)）\n    const ax = px + tw * 0.5 - (variant === 'yoyo' ? 6 : 0);\n    const ay = py + th * 0.5;\n    drawSegment(ctx, tex, ax, ay, rot, segLen, colorFn()); // :27907/:35205\n  }\n}\n\n// ===================== 两消费端入口 =====================\n\n/**\n * 悠悠球/配重球线（Main.cs:27799-27908，DrawProjDirect aiStyle 99 段）：\n * 基色 White、A 先 ×0.4（:27899-27900）→ TryApplyingPlayerStringColor →\n * 非 29 时 GetColor（alpha 重置 255，光照由合成自动）→ 全通道 ×0.5（:27902-27907）。\n */\nexport function drawYoyoStringLine(\n  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, tex: Tex,\n  startX: number, startY: number, target: StringLineTarget,\n  playerStringColor: number, opts: StringColorOpts,\n): void {\n  const colorFn = (): readonly [number, number, number, number] => {\n    // :27899-27901 White + A×0.4 → TryApplying\n    let c = tryApplyingPlayerStringColor(playerStringColor, [255, 255, 255, 102], opts);\n    // :27903-27906 GetColor：非 29 → alpha 重置 255（光照=合成自动乘，此处只重置 A）\n    if (playerStringColor !== 29) c = [c[0], c[1], c[2], 255];\n    // :27907 全通道 ×0.5（(byte) 截断）\n    return [btrunc(c[0] * 0.5), btrunc(c[1] * 0.5), btrunc(c[2] * 0.5), btrunc(c[3] * 0.5)];\n  };\n  runSegmentChain({ ctx, tex, startX, startY, target, colorFn, variant: 'yoyo' });\n}\n\n/**\n * 钓鱼线（Main.cs:34967-35205）：基色=竿色（:34988-35010 (200,200,200,100)+逐竿覆写）\n * → TryApplyingPlayerStringColor → 段色 GetColor（alpha 重置 255；29 免取光取原始色，\n * :35199-35204）。段链摆垂带 lai0 张力收敛（:35174-35175）。\n */\nexport function drawFishingStringLine(\n  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, tex: Tex,\n  startX: number, startY: number, target: StringLineTarget,\n  baseColor: readonly [number, number, number],\n  playerStringColor: number, opts: StringColorOpts,\n): void {\n  const colorFn = (): readonly [number, number, number, number] => {\n    // :34988-35013 基色 (rgb,100) → TryApplying\n    const c = tryApplyingPlayerStringColor(playerStringColor,\n      [baseColor[0], baseColor[1], baseColor[2], 100], opts);\n    // :35199-35204 GetColor alpha 重置 255（29 免取光：原始色 A=0 直用）\n    if (playerStringColor === 29) return c;\n    return [c[0], c[1], c[2], 255];\n  };\n  runSegmentChain({ ctx, tex, startX, startY, target, colorFn, variant: 'fishing' });\n}\n"
}

[0, 0];\n  let px = owner.cx + ox * dir - (dir < 0 ? 13 : 0);\n  let py = owner.cy + oy;\n  // 目标 = 浮标中心（:35112-35114）\n  let num = b.cx - px;\n  let num2 = b.cy - py;\n  if (num === 0 && num2 === 0) return;\n  {   // :35120-35132 竿尖回退 12px 再重算方向\n    const d = Math.hypot(num, num2);\n    const k = 12 / d;\n    px -= num * k; py -= num2 * k;\n    num = b.cx - px; num2 = b.cy - py;\n  }\n  const base = POLE_STRING_COLOR[b.poleVid] ?? [200, 200, 200];\n  ctx.globalAlpha = 100 / 255;\n  ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${base[0]},${base[1]},${base[2]})`;\n  let guard = 256;\n  while (guard-- > 0) {\n    let segLen = 12;\n    const dist = Math.hypot(num, num2);\n    const dist0 = dist;\n    if (dist < 20) { segLen = dist - 8; if (segLen <= 0) break; }\n    const k = 12 / dist;\n    px += num * k; py += num2 * k;\n    num = b.cx - px;\n    num2 = b.y + b.h * 0.1 - py;      // :35147 段目标 = 盒顶 10% 高处（线接浮标顶）\n    // 摆垂（:35150-35190,仅段长>12 时）\n    if (dist0 > 12) {\n      let sway = 0.3;\n      let spd = Math.abs(b.vx) + Math.abs(b.vy);\n      if (spd > 16) spd = 16;\n      sway *= 1 - spd / 16;\n      let f = dist0 / 80;\n      if (f > 1) f = 1;\n      sway *= f;\n      sway *= 1 - b.lai0 / 100;\n      if (num2 > 0) {\n        num2 *= 1 + sway;\n        num *= 1 - sway;\n      } else {\n        f = Math.abs(b.vx) / 3;\n        if (f > 1) f = 1;\n        f -= 0.5;\n        sway *= f;\n        if (sway > 0) sway *= 2;\n        num2 *= 1 + sway;\n        num *= 1 - sway;\n      }\n    }\n    const ang = Math.atan2(num2, num) - Math.PI / 2;\n    // 贴竖条（:35194-35199）：origin=(W/2,0),从段起点沿方向延伸 segLen\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(px, py);\n    ctx.rotate(ang);\n    ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, 0, tex.width, Math.round(segLen), -tex.width / 2, 0, tex.width, Math.round(segLen));\n    ctx.restore();\n    if (dist < 20) break;\n  }\n  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n}",

[0, 0];\n  const px = owner.cx + ox * dir - (dir < 0 ? 13 : 0);\n  const py = owner.cy + oy;\n  const base = POLE_STRING_COLOR[b.poleVid] ?? [200, 200, 200];\n  drawFishingStringLine(ctx, tex, px, py,\n    { x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, lai0: b.lai0 },\n    base, owner.equipStats.stringColor, stringColorOpts(r.lighting?.clock));\n}"

  create: number;          // 原版 item id
  createStack: number;
  tile: number;           // 站点 TileID（-1=徒手）
  items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number }>;
  groups: number[];
  honey?: boolean; water?: boolean; lava?: boolean;
  snow?: boolean; graveyard?: boolean; alchemy?: boolean;
  /** decraft 族(2026-08-13 提取器补提,Recipe.cs 裸赋值 :1064/:1140/:1145) */

  crimson?: boolean;
  corruption?: boolean;
  /** AddCustomShimmerResult(:173)——decraft 产物覆盖表 [[id,stack],...] */
  shimmer?: Array<[number, number]>;
}

  count: number;

};

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-19T07:52:40.395Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
  ensureVImage(file: string): ImageBitmap | ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
    if (hit) {
      // 消费登记(2026-08-19 素材重制热补丁):烘焙期命中(hit)的表也要登记到
      // ChunkCache.chunkConsumed——替换已就位 sheet 的精确重烘依据(onBakeAssetArrived
      // 只覆盖 miss 晚到链,对已就位表是 no-op)。可选方法:无 ChunkCache 的环境安全
      if (this.bakeTracker?._baking) this.bakeTracker.noteConsumed?.(file);
      return hit;
    }
    // ★note 先于 pending/failed 早退(2026-08-19 用户实报"生命树贴图不及时,
    // 手动破坏才渲染"):加载已在飞行中(预载/他人发起)时烘焙期 ensure 会早退,
    // 曾把 note 一起吞掉 → 表晚到无人重烘 = 缺表 fallback 钉死。failed 同 note:
    // 重试成功会二次 land → onLoaded → 重烘,链路反而闭环
    if (this.bakeTracker?._baking) this.bakeTracker.note(file);
    if (this._iconPending.has(file) || this._vImageFailed.has(file)) return null;
    this._iconPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => {
      // ImageBitmap 桥(2026-08-14 根治):入表即自持已解码像素;晚到/烘焙
      // 钩子在 bitmap 落地后触发(消费方读表内对象)
      const land = (store: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => {
        this.vimages.set(file, store);
        this._iconPending.delete(file);
        this._vImageFailed.delete(file);
        if (this.bakeTracker) this.bakeTracker.onLoaded(file);
        this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
      };
      if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) land(im);
      else tryBitmapUpgrade(im, file, land, () => land(im));  // 失败回退+退避重试(重试成功二次 land=晚到重烘,安全)
    };
    im.onerror = () => {
      this._iconPending.delete(file);
      this._vImageFailed.add(file);   // 永久失败:不再重复请求,供消费方分级告警
    };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
    return null;
  }
  /** 表是否已确认加载失败(404 等)——与"加载中瞬态"区分 */
  vImageFailed(file: string): boolean { return this._vImageFailed.has(file); }

```

XNA: EntitySpriteDraw(texture, position, sourceRectangle, color, rotation, origin, scale, effects) where:

The sprite's texture-space point `origin` (6, 0) maps to `position`. In world space: position + screenPosition = vector + (6, 6). So texture point (6,0) lands at world (vector.X + 6, vector.Y + 6).

function drawFishingLine(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, r: import('../render/Renderer').Renderer,
  b: Bobber, owner: import('./Player').Player,
): void {
  const tex = r.atlas?.ensureVImage?.('vanilla/FishingLine.png');
  if (!tex) return;
  // 竿尖 = 玩家中心 + 逐竿偏移（:34970-35106;dir<0 再 −13）
  const dir = owner.facing || 1;
  const [ox, oy] = POLE_TIP[b.poleVid] ?? [0, 0];
  const px = owner.cx + ox * dir - (dir < 0 ? 13 : 0);
  const py = owner.cy + oy;
  const base = POLE_STRING_COLOR[b.poleVid] ?? [200, 200, 200];
  drawFishingStringLine(ctx, tex, px, py,
    { x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h, vx: b.vx, vy: b.vy, lai0: b.lai0 },
    base, owner.equipStats.stringColor, stringColorOpts(r.lighting?.clock));
}

45	export function stringColorOpts(clock: StringColorClock | null | undefined): StringColorOpts {

52	/** C# (byte)float 截断（正数 = floor）；(byte)int 非检查上下文回绕 mod 256 */
53	const btrunc = (v: number): number => Math.trunc(v) & 0xff;
54	
55	/** Main.rand.Next(min, max)（左闭右开） */
56	function nextInt(rand: () => number, min: number, max: number): number {
57	  return min + Math.floor(rand() * (max - min));
58	}

64	 * (byte)int 回绕语义）且 A=0（预乘 = 纯加色）。playerStringColor ≤ 0 原色透传。
65	 */
66	export function tryApplyingPlayerStringColor(
67	  playerStringColor: number,
68	  stringColor: readonly [number, number, number, number],
69	  opts: StringColorOpts,
70	): [number, number, number, number] {
71	  let [r, g, b, a] = stringColor;
72	  if (playerStringColor > 0) {
73	    let pc = paintColor(playerStringColor);              // :34916 WorldGen.paintColor
74	    r = pc[0]; g = pc[1]; b = pc[2]; a = pc[3];
75	    if (r < 75) r = 75;                                  // :34917-34928 RGB 下限
76	    if (g < 75) g = 75;
77	    if (b < 75) b = 75;
78	    switch (playerStringColor) {                         // :34929-34947 特例覆写（A=255）
79	      case 13: r = 20; g = 20; b = 20; a = 255; break;
80	      case 0: case 14: r = 200; g = 200; b = 200; a = 255; break;
81	      case 28: r = 163; g = 116; b = 91; a = 255; break;
82	      case 27: r = opts.disco[0]; g = opts.disco[1]; b = opts.disco[2]; a = 255; break;
83	      case 30: r = 226; g = 228; b = 142; a = 255; break;
84	    }
85	    a = btrunc(a * 0.4);                                 // :34948 A×0.4
86	    if (playerStringColor === 29) {                      // :34949-34962 鼠标文字色闪烁
87	      const rand = opts.rand ?? Math.random;
88	      let num = (opts.mouseTextColor - 190) / 65;        // :34951
89	      num += nextInt(rand, -100, 101) * 0.005;           // :34952
90	      num = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, num));               // :34953 Utils.Clamp
91	      const num2 = 1 - num;                              // :34954
92	      let rb = btrunc(184 * num + 255 * num2);           // :34955-34957
93	      let gb = btrunc(116 * num + 184 * num2);
94	      let bb = btrunc(255 * num + 116 * num2);
95	      rb = btrunc(rb + nextInt(rand, -40, 41));          // :34958-34960 (byte)int 回绕
96	      gb = btrunc(gb + nextInt(rand, -40, 41));
97	      bb = btrunc(bb + nextInt(rand, -40, 41));
98	      r = rb; g = gb; b = bb; a = 0;                     // :34961 A=0（预乘 = 纯加色）
99	    }
100	  }
101	  return [r, g, b, a];

104	// ===================== 染色贴图（预乘语义三档消费） =====================
105	
106	type Tex = ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement;
107	
108	/** FishingLine 染色缓存：键=源贴图+RGB+档（保形/摊平）。29 号闪烁每段独立色，
109	 *  缓存打满即清（sparkleTintCache 同策略；12×12 画布极小）。 */
110	const tintCache = new WeakMap<Tex, Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>>();
111	
112	/** 染色：multiply 乘 RGB；flatten=false 时 destination-in 回贴原 alpha（保形），
113	 *  flatten=true 时摊平 alpha=255（'lighter' 加色用——透明像素成不透明黑，加 0 无害）。
114	 *  （ChunkCache.tintRegion 同款三段合成） */
115	function tintedTex(tex: Tex, rgb: readonly [number, number, number], flatten: boolean): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
116	  const w = (tex as { width: number }).width, h = (tex as { height: number }).height;
117	  if (!w || !h) return null;
118	  let m = tintCache.get(tex);
119	  if (!m) { m = new Map(); tintCache.set(tex, m); }
120	  const key = `${rgb[0]},${rgb[1]},${rgb[2]}|${flatten ? 1 : 0}`;
121	  const hit = m.get(key);
122	  if (hit) return hit;
123	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
124	  c.width = w; c.height = h;
125	  const cx = c.getContext('2d');
126	  if (!cx) return null;
127	  cx.drawImage(tex as CanvasImageSource, 0, 0);
128	  cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';               // rgb = 贴图.rgb × 染色
129	  cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${rgb[0]},${rgb[1]},${rgb[2]})`;
130	  cx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
131	  if (!flatten) {                                         // 回贴原 alpha 形状
132	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
133	    cx.drawImage(tex as CanvasImageSource, 0, 0);
134	  }
135	  cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
136	  if (m.size > 64) m.clear();
137	  m.set(key, c);
138	  return c;
139	}
140	
141	/**
142	 * 单段线绘制（预乘混合精确等价，见文件头）：(x,y)=origin 落点（XNA position 语义），
143	 * rot=段角，segLen=段长（(int) 截断，Main.cs:27907/:35205 sourceRectangle 高）。
144	 */
145	function drawSegment(
146	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, tex: Tex,
147	  x: number, y: number, rot: number, segLen: number,
148	  color: readonly [number, number, number, number],
149	): void {
150	  const h = Math.trunc(segLen);                           // Rectangle (int)num5
151	  if (h <= 0) return;
152	  const w = (tex as { width: number }).width;
153	  const a = color[3];
154	  ctx.save();
155	  ctx.translate(x, y);
156	  ctx.rotate(rot);
157	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
158	  if (a === 0) {
159	    // 纯加色：摊平染色 + 'lighter'（A=0 预乘 = 背景零衰减）
160	    const t = tintedTex(tex, [color[0], color[1], color[2]], true);
161	    if (!t) { ctx.restore(); return; }
162	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
163	    ctx.drawImage(t, 0, 0, w, h, -w / 2, 0, w, h);
164	  } else if (a >= 255) {
165	    // 纯替换：保形染色 source-over（钓线 GetColor alpha 重置 255 路径）
166	    const t = tintedTex(tex, [color[0], color[1], color[2]], false);
167	    if (!t) { ctx.restore(); return; }
168	    ctx.drawImage(t, 0, 0, w, h, -w / 2, 0, w, h);
169	  } else {
170	    // 中间档两笔：黑形压暗背景(×(1−A/255)) + 摊平染色 'lighter' 补贡献
171	    const black = tintedTex(tex, [0, 0, 0], false);
172	    const t = tintedTex(tex, [color[0], color[1], color[2]], true);
173	    if (!black || !t) { ctx.restore(); return; }
174	    ctx.globalAlpha = a / 255;
175	    ctx.drawImage(black, 0, 0, w, h, -w / 2, 0, w, h);
176	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
177	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
178	    ctx.drawImage(t, 0, 0, w, h, -w / 2, 0, w, h);
179	  }
180	  ctx.restore();
181	}
182	
183	// ===================== 段链（两消费端共享的 12px 段循环） =====================
184	
185	/** 段链目标（Projectile 盒 + 速度；钓鱼线另带 lai0=localAI[0] 张力 0-100） */
186	export interface StringLineTarget {
187	  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
188	  vx: number; vy: number;
189	  lai0?: number;
190	}
191	
192	/** 每段终色回调（消费端闭包：基色 → TryApplying → GetColor alpha 重置/×0.5） */
193	type SegmentColorFn = () => readonly [number, number, number, number];
194	
195	interface ChainArgs {
196	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D;
197	  tex: Tex;
198	  /** 线起点（悠悠球=MountedCenter / 钓线=竿尖） */
199	  startX: number; startY: number;
200	  target: StringLineTarget;
201	  colorFn: SegmentColorFn;
202	  /** 悠悠球（'yoyo'）与钓线（'fishing'）的三处差异：
203	   *  起步回退 ×0.1 vs 全 12px（:27820/:35127）；首段不推进 vs 恒推进（:27843-27851）；
204	   *  摆垂 num8 ×f×0.5 vs ×(1−lai0/100)（:27874-27875/:35174-35175）；
205	   *  origin 落点 −(6,0) vs 无（:27907/:35205） */
206	  variant: 'yoyo' | 'fishing';
207	}
208	
209	/** 段链主体（钓线 :35112-35205 / 悠悠球线 :27801-27908 同构循环）：
210	 *  12px 段沿起点→目标推进，速度/张力摆垂（num8），逐段旋贴 FishingLine 竖条。
211	 *  guard 上限为防御性护栏（原版无；终止条件 = 段距 <20 走尾段）。 */
212	function runSegmentChain(a: ChainArgs): void {
213	  const { ctx, tex, target, colorFn, variant } = a;
214	  const tw = (tex as { width: number }).width;
215	  const th = (tex as { height: number }).height;
216	  let px = a.startX, py = a.startY;
217	  let num = target.x + target.w * 0.5 - px;               // :27804/:35112 目标=盒中心
218	  let num2 = target.y + target.h * 0.5 - py;
219	  let flag = true;
220	  let flag2 = true;                                       // :27809 悠悠球首段不推进
221	  if (num === 0 && num2 === 0) {
222	    flag = false;                                         // :27810/:35117
223	  } else {
224	    const k = 12 / Math.hypot(num, num2);                 // :27816-27819/:35123-35126
225	    num *= k; num2 *= k;
226	    if (variant === 'yoyo') {                             // :27820-27821 起步回退 ×0.1
227	      px -= num * 0.1; py -= num2 * 0.1;
228	    } else {                                              // :35127-35128 全 12px 回退
229	      px -= num; py -= num2;
230	    }
231	    num = target.x + target.w * 0.5 - px;
232	    num2 = target.y + target.h * 0.5 - py;
233	  }
234	  let guard = 512;
235	  while (flag && guard-- > 0) {
236	    let segLen = 12;                                      // :27827/:35134
237	    const dist = Math.hypot(num, num2);
238	    const dist0 = dist;                                   // num7
239	    if (Number.isNaN(dist)) { flag = false; continue; }   // :27830-27834/:35137-35141
240	    if (dist < 20) { segLen = dist - 8; flag = false; }   // :27835-27839/:35142-35146
241	    const k = 12 / dist;
242	    num *= k; num2 *= k;
243	    if (variant === 'yoyo') {                             // :27843-27851 首段不推进
244	      if (flag2) flag2 = false;
245	      else { px += num; py += num2; }
246	    } else {                                              // :35150-35151 恒推进
247	      px += num; py += num2;
248	    }
249	    num = target.x + target.w * 0.5 - px;
250	    num2 = target.y + target.h * 0.1 - py;                // :27852-27853/:35152-35153 段目标=盒顶 10%
251	    if (dist0 > 12) {                                     // :27854/:35154 摆垂（仅段距 >12）
252	      let sway = 0.3;
253	      let spd = Math.abs(target.vx) + Math.abs(target.vy);
254	      if (spd > 16) spd = 16;
255	      sway *= 1 - spd / 16;                               // :27862-27863
256	      let f = dist0 / 80;
257	      if (f > 1) f = 1;
258	      sway *= f;                                          // :27869/:35169
259	      if (sway < 0) sway = 0;                             // :27870-27873
260	      if (variant === 'yoyo') {
261	        sway *= f;                                        // :27874 悠悠球二次 ×f
262	        sway *= 0.5;                                      // :27875 ×0.5
263	      } else {
264	        sway *= 1 - (target.lai0 ?? 0) / 100;             // :35174-35175 张力收敛
265	      }
266	      if (num2 > 0) {                                     // :27876-27880/:35176-35180
267	        num2 *= 1 + sway;
268	        num *= 1 - sway;
269	      } else {                                            // :27881-27896/:35181-35196
270	        f = Math.abs(target.vx) / 3;
271	        if (f > 1) f = 1;
272	        f -= 0.5;
273	        sway *= f;
274	        if (sway > 0) sway *= 2;
275	        num2 *= 1 + sway;
276	        num *= 1 - sway;
277	      }
278	    }
279	    const rot = Math.atan2(num2, num) - Math.PI / 2;      // :27898/:35198
280	    // origin 落点（XNA position−screenPos+(W/2,H/2)：悠悠球线再 −(6,0)）
281	    const ax = px + tw * 0.5 - (variant === 'yoyo' ? 6 : 0);
282	    const ay = py + th * 0.5;
283	    drawSegment(ctx, tex, ax, ay, rot, segLen, colorFn()); // :27907/:35205
284	  }
285	}
286	
287	// ===================== 两消费端入口 =====================
288	
289	/**
290	 * 悠悠球/配重球线（Main.cs:27799-27908，DrawProjDirect aiStyle 99 段）：
291	 * 基色 White、A 先 ×0.4（:27899-27900）→ TryApplyingPlayerStringColor →
292	 * 非 29 时 GetColor（alpha 重置 255，光照由合成自动）→ 全通道 ×0.5（:27902-27907）。
293	 */
294	export function drawYoyoStringLine(
295	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, tex: Tex,
296	  startX: number, startY: number, target: StringLineTarget,
297	  playerStringColor: number, opts: StringColorOpts,
298	): void {
299	  const colorFn = (): readonly [number, number, number, number] => {
300	    // :27899-27901 White + A×0.4 → TryApplying
301	    let c = tryApplyingPlayerStringColor(playerStringColor, [255, 255, 255, 102], opts);
302	    // :27903-27906 GetColor：非 29 → alpha 重置 255（光照=合成自动乘，此处只重置 A）
303	    if (playerStringColor !== 29) c = [c[0], c[1], c[2], 255];
304	    // :27907 全通道 ×0.5（(byte) 截断）
305	    return [btrunc(c[0] * 0.5), btrunc(c[1] * 0.5), btrunc(c[2] * 0.5), btrunc(c[3] * 0.5)];
306	  };
307	  runSegmentChain({ ctx, tex, startX, startY, target, colorFn, variant: 'yoyo' });
308	}
309	
310	/**
311	 * 钓鱼线（Main.cs:34967-35205）：基色=竿色（:34988-35010 (200,200,200,100)+逐竿覆写）
312	 * → TryApplyingPlayerStringColor → 段色 GetColor（alpha 重置 255；29 免取光取原始色，
313	 * :35199-35204）。段链摆垂带 lai0 张力收敛（:35174-35175）。
314	 */
315	export function drawFishingStringLine(
316	  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, tex: Tex,
317	  startX: number, startY: number, target: StringLineTarget,
318	  baseColor: readonly [number, number, number],
319	  playerStringColor: number, opts: StringColorOpts,
320	): void {
321	  const colorFn = (): readonly [number, number, number, number] => {
322	    // :34988-35013 基色 (rgb,100) → TryApplying
323	    const c = tryApplyingPlayerStringColor(playerStringColor,
324	      [baseColor[0], baseColor[1], baseColor[2], 100], opts);
325	    // :35199-35204 GetColor alpha 重置 255（29 免取光：原始色 A=0 直用）
326	    if (playerStringColor === 29) return c;
327	    return [c[0], c[1], c[2], 255];
328	  };
329	  runSegmentChain({ ctx, tex, startX, startY, target, colorFn, variant: 'fishing' });
330	}
331	

```

## 关键裁决（源码为准）
